  1. [2608.28636] 三维回旋曲线的显式笛卡尔坐标
    Explicit Cartesian Coordinates for Three-Dimensional Clothoids
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文旨在为三维回旋曲线提供显式坐标。
    • 方法结合了球面回旋曲线重构定理、自旋参数化和Kampé de Fériet积分求值。
    • 主要结果是为所有非螺旋三维回旋曲线给出了显式笛卡尔坐标。
    • 此外，利用Kummer渐近性获得了显式渐近线及其性质。
    • 该工作为回旋曲线的计算和应用提供了新的工具。
    💡 结合球面回旋曲线重构定理与自旋参数化，给出所有非螺旋三维回旋曲线的显式弧长参数坐标。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们将Lucas和Ortega-Yagues的球面回旋曲线重构定理与球面回旋曲线的显式自旋参数化以及Kampé de Fériet对Kummer乘积积分的求值相结合。这为每一个非螺旋三维回旋曲线提供了以弧长为参数的显式笛卡尔坐标。Kummer渐近性进而给出显式渐近线，包括其gamma-digamma偏移量以及它们轴之间的夹角。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We combine the spherical-clothoid reconstruction theorem of Lucas and Ortega-Yagues with an explicit spinorial parametrization of the spherical clothoid and a Kampe de Feriet evaluation of Kummer-product integrals. This gives explicit Cartesian coordinates, in arclength, for every nonhelical three-dimensional clothoid. Kummer asymptotics then yield explicit asymptotic lines, including their gamma-digamma offsets and the angle between their axes.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28636
  2. [2608.28793] 关于完备极大映射及其奇点
    On the complete maximal maps and their singularities
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究极大曲面的全局结构。
    • 关注亏格、奇分支数量和简单末端之间的关系。
    • 主要结果是构造了任意亏格和任意多奇分支的完备极大映射。
    • 这有助于理解极大曲面的奇点行为。
    💡 研究Lorentz-Minkowski空间中极大曲面的全局结构，构造任意亏格具有任意多奇分支的完备极大映射。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究了Lorentz-Minkowski 3-空间$\mathbb{E}^3_1$中极大曲面（具有零平均曲率的类空浸入）的全局结构，特别关注亏格、奇分支轨迹数量与简单末端之间的相互作用。我们构造了具有任意多奇分支的完备极大映射，对于任何亏格$p\geq 0$且具有简单末端。

    🌐 English Abstract

    This article investigates the global structure of maximal surfaces (space-like immersions with zero mean curvature) in Lorentz-Minkowski $3$-space $\mathbb{E}^3_1$, especially focusing on the interplay between genus, the number of singular components--loci, and simple ends. We construct complete maximal maps with arbitrarily many singular components for any genus $p\geq 0$ with simple ends.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28793
  3. [2608.29086] 蛋白质相互作用的新数学模型（二）：模型背后的数学
    A novel mathematical model of protein interactions (2): The mathematics behind the model
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文是蛋白质相互作用模型的续篇，解决两个基本问题。
    • 问题一是如何定义分子形状，利用范畴论给出定义方程组。
    • 问题二是如何判断环内孔洞是否奇异，利用上同调理论给出判定方程。
    • 该工作为蛋白质建模提供了数学基础。
    💡 用范畴论和上同调理论回答分子形状定义及环内孔洞奇异性判定问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文是“从电子离域角度看蛋白质相互作用的新数学模型（2025）”的续篇，其中蛋白质分子被建模为内部无奇异孔洞的三角形环。这里我们考虑两个问题：（1）如何定义分子的形状？（即如何定义内部无奇异孔洞的环的形状？），以及（2）给定的环是分子吗？（即环内的孔洞是奇异的吗？）。这些问题分别通过（1）使用范畴论的概念给出分子形状的定义方程组，以及（2）使用上同调理论的概念给出确定环内孔洞是否奇异的方程来回答。不需要之前论文、范畴论或上同调理论的先验知识。

    🌐 English Abstract

    This article is a sequel to ``A novel mathematical model of protein interactions from the perspective of electron delocalization (2025)'', where protein molecules are modelded as loops of triangles with no singular holes inside. Here we consider two questions: (1) How can we define the shape of a molecule? (i.e., how can we define the shape of a loop with no singular holes inside?), and (2) Is the given loop a molecule? (i.e., are the holes within a loop are singular?). These questions are answered by (1) giving a defining system of equations for the shape of a molecule using concepts from category theory, and (2) giving a equation that determines whether holes within a loop are singular using concepts from cohomology theory, respectively. No prior knowledge of the previous paper, category theory, or cohomology theory is required.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29086
  4. [2608.29148] 与自对偶Einstein ACH度量相关的CR 3-流形的环境与Poincaré度量
    Ambient and Poincar\'e metrics for CR $3$-manifolds associated with the self-dual Einstein ACH metric
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文旨在构造CR流形的新环境与Poincaré度量。
    • 方法是从自对偶Einstein ACH度量出发，而非经典近似解。
    • 新度量满足Einstein-Maxwell型方程，且确定到无限阶。
    • 这允许构造高阶CR不变算子和不变量。
    • 应用包括所有阶CR GJMS算子的环境度量构造。
    💡 从自对偶Einstein ACH度量构造CR流形的新环境与Poincaré度量，满足Einstein-Maxwell型方程，并应用于CR GJMS算子。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们从相关的自对偶Einstein ACH度量出发，为3维CR流形上的Fefferman共形流形构造新的环境与Poincaré度量。与由复Monge-Ampère方程的近似解产生的经典环境与Poincaré度量不同，这些度量满足无限阶的Einstein-Maxwell型方程，而不是Ricci平坦或Einstein方程。由于这些度量无歧义地确定到无限阶，它们使我们能够构造涉及Tanaka-Webster曲率和挠率的任意高阶导数的CR不变微分算子和局部CR不变量。作为应用，我们获得了3维中所有阶CR GJMS（Gover-Graham）算子的环境度量构造。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We construct new ambient and Poincar\'e metrics for the Fefferman conformal manifold over $3$-dimensional CR manifolds, starting from the associated self-dual Einstein ACH metric. Unlike the classical ambient and Poincar\'e metrics arising from approximate solutions to the complex Monge-Amp\`ere equation, these metrics satisfy Einstein-Maxwell-type equations to infinite order rather than the Ricci-flat or Einstein equations. Since these metrics are determined to infinite order without ambiguity, they enable us to construct CR invariant differential operators and local CR invariants involving arbitrarily high order derivatives of the Tanaka-Webster curvature and torsion. As an application, we obtain the ambient metric construction of the CR GJMS (Gover-Graham) operators of all orders in dimension $3$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29148
  5. [2608.29298] 关于空间形式中作为梯度Yamabe孤子的超曲面
    On Hypersurfaces of Space Forms That Are Gradient Yamabe Solitons
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究空间形式中的梯度Yamabe孤子超曲面。
    • 通过分析水平集给出结构刻画。
    • 在无迹第二基本形式条件下，证明孤子要么分解为乘积，要么满足下界。
    • 下界达到等价于平行子流形乘积。
    💡 研究空间形式中常数量曲率的完备浸入超曲面作为梯度Yamabe孤子的结构，给出分解或下界刻画。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究了在空间形式$\left(\overline{M}^n(C), \overline{g}\right)$中作为具有常数量曲率的完备浸入超曲面出现的非平凡Yamabe梯度孤子。对于具有非消失梯度的孤子，我们通过分析孤子函数的正则水平集$\Sigma$建立了孤子的结构刻画。特别地，对$\Sigma$作为空间形式子流形的第二基本形式的无迹部分$\Phi^{\Sigma}_{\overline{M}}$施加适当条件，我们证明该孤子要么分解为$\mathbb{R}$与空间形式的全脐子流形的黎曼积，要么满足$\sup\limits_\Sigma\left|\Phi^{\Sigma}_{\overline{M}}\right|$的尖锐下界。此外，我们证明下界达到当且仅当孤子等距于$\mathbb{R}$与空间形式的平行子流形的黎曼积。

    🌐 English Abstract

    This paper studies nontrivial Yamabe gradient solitons occurring as complete immersed hypersurfaces with constant scalar curvature in space forms $\left(\overline{M}^n(C), \overline{g}\right)$. For solitons with non-vanishing gradients, we establish a structural characterization of the soliton by analyzing a regular level set $\Sigma$ of the soliton function. In particular, imposing a suitable condition on the traceless part of the second fundamental form, $\Phi^{\Sigma}_{\overline{M}}$ of $\Sigma$ as a submanifold of the space form, we prove that the soliton either decomposes as a Riemannian product of $\mathbb{R}$ and a totally umbilical submanifold of the space form, or satisfies a sharp lower bound on $\sup\limits_\Sigma\left|\Phi^{\Sigma}_{\overline{M}}\right|$. Furthermore, we show that the lower bound is attained if, and only if, the soliton is isometric to a Riemannian product of $\mathbb{R}$ and a parallel submanifold of the space form.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29298

    📖 中文摘要

    我们给出了在$S^1 \times S^{n-1}$（$n$为偶数）上具有标准Lorentz度量$g=-g_{S^1}+g_{S^{n-1}}$的旋量丛上交织算子的谱的显式公式。作为特例，我们构造了所有奇数阶的共形协变微分算子，其首项为Dirac算子的幂。

    🌐 English Abstract

  7. [2608.29318] 共形乘积的Faraday形式：Weyl曲率与四维Einstein刚性
    The Faraday Form of Conformal Products: Weyl Curvature and Four-Dimensional Einstein Rigidity
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究共形乘积结构的Faraday形式。
    • 将Faraday形式用Weyl曲率表示，无需Ricci假设。
    • 推导了Lee形式的恒等式。
    • 在四维Einstein情形证明了刚性，万有覆盖为常曲率曲面乘积。
    💡 将共形乘积结构的Faraday形式用Weyl曲率表示，推导Lee形式恒等式，证明四维紧致Einstein流形刚性。
    📖 中文摘要

    设$(M^n,g)$具有共形乘积结构，其适配Weyl联络$D$保持秩为$p,q\ge2$的正交分布。我们直接用$g$的Weyl曲率表示Faraday形式$d\theta$。若$S$是由分裂确定的正交对合，且$\mathcal K_W(X)=\sum_i W_{X,e_i}(Se_i)$对任意局部标准正交标架成立，则$(d\theta)^\sharp = \frac{n-2}{4(p-1)(q-1)}[\mathcal K_W,S]$。不需要Ricci曲率假设。相同的曲率计算给出$\nabla\theta$对称部分的伴随公式，$S\Theta^s-\Theta^sS+\theta^\sharp\wedge S\theta^\sharp = \frac{p-q}{n-2}(d\theta)^\sharp +\frac1{n-2}[S,\Ric^\sharp]$，其中$\Theta=\nabla\theta$。当Ricci张量关于乘积分裂为块对角时，这些公式导致Lee形式两个分量的显式恒等式。在四维中，秩$(2,2)$分裂确定一个ambi-Hermitian对。经典ambi-Hermitian曲率恒等式在Ricci张量对两个复结构不变时蕴含局部闭性；我们也从上述混合Weyl迹恢复此结论。最后，若$(M^4,g)$紧致且Einstein，则无需特殊假设：$D=\nabla^g$。因此万有覆盖是两个具有相同常Gauss曲率的单连通曲面的乘积。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $(M^n,g)$ carry a conformal product structure whose adapted Weyl connection $D$ preserves orthogonal distributions of ranks $p,q\ge2$. We express the Faraday form $d\theta$ directly in terms of the Weyl curvature of $g$. If $S$ is the orthogonal involution determined by the splitting and \[ \mathcal K_W(X)=\sum_i W_{X,e_i}(Se_i) \] for any local orthonormal frame, then \[ (d\theta)^\sharp = \frac{n-2}{4(p-1)(q-1)}[\mathcal K_W,S]. \] No Ricci-curvature assumption is required. The same curvature calculation gives a companion formula for the symmetric part of $\nabla\theta$, \[ S\Theta^s-\Theta^sS+\theta^\sharp\wedge S\theta^\sharp = \frac{p-q}{n-2}(d\theta)^\sharp +\frac1{n-2}[S,\Ric^\sharp], \qquad \Theta=\nabla\theta. \] When the Ricci tensor is block diagonal with respect to the product splitting, these formulas lead to explicit identities for the two components of the Lee form. In dimension four, the rank-$(2,2)$ splitting determines an ambi-Hermitian pair. The classical ambi-Hermitian curvature identities imply local closedness when the Ricci tensor is invariant under both complex structures; we also recover this conclusion from the mixed Weyl trace above. Finally, if $(M^4,g)$ is compact and Einstein, no specialness assumption is needed: $D=\nabla^g$. Hence the universal cover is the product of two simply connected surfaces with the same constant Gaussian curvature.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29318
  8. [2608.29406] Sasakian流形的部分全实子流形
    Partially Totally Real Submanifolds of Sasakian Manifolds
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文推广PTR子流形到Sasakian流形。
    • 定义了全实和歧义分布，并证明多种子流形为特例。
    • 建立了切向法向分解，研究了可积性等性质。
    • 探讨了典范态射和空间形式中的例子。
    💡 将Kähler几何中的部分全实子流形推广到Sasakian流形，建立框架并研究特例。
    📖 中文摘要

    部分全实（PTR）子流形在Kähler几何中通过区分全实分布并保持其正交补无限制而引入。在本文中，我们为Sasakian流形中与Reeb向量场相切的子流形建立了相应的框架。在分离Reeb方向后，我们定义了全实分布和歧义分布，并表明反不变、接触CR、半倾斜和逐点半倾斜子流形作为Sasakian PTR框架的特例出现。我们建立了基本的切向和法向分解，研究了极大性和可积性，并推导了由Reeb场产生的额外限制。然后我们研究了典范态射$P$和$F$、相关分布的几何，以及Sasakian空间形式中的PTR子流形。包含显式模型以说明主要结构和与Kähler情形的差异。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Partially totally real (PTR) submanifolds were introduced in K\"ahler geometry by distinguishing a totally real distribution and leaving its orthogonal complement unrestricted. In this paper we develop the corresponding framework for submanifolds of Sasakian manifolds tangent to the Reeb vector field. After separating the Reeb direction, we define the totally real and ambiguous distributions and show that anti-invariant, contact CR, hemi-slant and pointwise hemi-slant submanifolds occur as special cases of the Sasakian PTR framework. We establish the basic tangential and normal decompositions, study maximality and integrability, and derive the additional restrictions produced by the Reeb field. We then investigate the canonical morphisms \(P\) and \(F\), the geometry of the associated distributions, and PTR-submanifolds in Sasakian space forms. Explicit models are included to illustrate the principal structures and the differences from the K\"ahler case.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29406
  9. [2608.29427] 渐近平坦切丛的正质量与刚性
    Positive mass and rigidity for asymptotically flat tangent bundles
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究渐近平坦切丛上的正质量定理。
    • 构造了自然渐近平坦度量，但衰减慢于标准阈值。
    • 证明了广义质量良定义且由底层流形决定。
    • 获得了正质量定理和刚性定理。
    💡 对渐近平坦流形的切丛上自然渐近平坦度量证明正质量定理和刚性定理。
    📖 中文摘要

    设$(M,g)$是渐近平坦流形，其切丛$TM$微分同胚于渐近欧氏空间。我们证明了一类具有缓慢渐近衰减的渐近平坦度量$\widetilde g$在$TM$上的正质量定理和正质量刚性定理。这些度量通过在$TM$的水平分布上插值欧氏度量与无穷远处的基础度量$g$来构造。主要困难在于$TM$上的诱导度量在$2n$维中衰减低于ADM质量的标准阈值；然而，我们证明它们的渐近质量在广义意义上是良定义的，并由底层流形$M$上的几何数据决定。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $(M,g)$ be an asymptotically flat manifold such that its tangent bundle $TM$ is diffeomorphically asymptotically Euclidean. We prove a positive mass theorem and a positive mass rigidity theorem for a natural class of asymptotically flat metrics $\widetilde g$ on $TM$ exhibiting slow asymptotic decay. These metrics are constructed by interpolating, along the horizontal distribution of $TM$, between the Euclidean metric and the base metric $g$ near infinity. The main difficulty is that the induced metrics on $TM$ decay below the standard threshold for the ADM mass in dimension $2n$; nevertheless, we show that their asymptotic mass is well-defined in a generalized sense and is determined by geometric data on the underlying manifold $M$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29427
  10. [2608.29554] 一类Hermitian Yang-Mills度量的极限行为，II：指数逼近
    Limiting Behavior of a Class of Hermitian Yang--Mills Metrics, II: Exponential Approximation
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究近似HYM度量的指数逼近性质。
    • 证明了平均曲率在$C^k$范数下指数衰减。
    • 精确度量与近似度量的差在$C^k$范数下指数小。
    • 主要难点在于全局$C^0$估计。
    💡 证明近似Hermitian Yang-Mills度量在$C^k$范数下指数衰减，精确度量指数逼近。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文是[9]的续篇，其中第一作者在由双谱覆盖产生的稳定秩二全纯向量丛上构造了一族近似Hermitian Yang-Mills度量$H_{0,\epsilon}$，该覆盖位于两个一维复环面的乘积上。我们证明这些近似度量在大的Kähler极限下给出了精确Hermitian Yang-Mills度量的全阶、指数精确的渐近描述。更精确地，$H_{0,\epsilon}$的平均曲率在每个$C^k$范数下指数衰减。此外，若$H_{1,\epsilon}$表示精确Hermitian Yang-Mills度量且$H_\epsilon=H_{0,\epsilon}^{-1}H_{1,\epsilon}$，则归一化后，对每个非负整数$k$，存在正常数$C_k$和$c_k$使得$\|H_\epsilon- Id\|_{C^k}\leq C_k e^{-\frac{c_{k}}{\epsilon}}$。主要分析难点在于全局$C^0$比较。获得耦合非线性Hermitian Yang-Mills系统的$C^0$估计本质上是困难的；此外，方程仅控制曲率的收缩，因此仅控制二阶导数的某些组合，而需要全局控制整个矩阵值度量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    This paper is a sequel to [9], where the first-named author constructed a family of approximate Hermitian Yang--Mills metrics $H_{0,\epsilon}$ on stable rank-two holomorphic vector bundles arising from double spectral covers over the product of two one-dimensional complex tori. We prove that these approximate metrics give an all-order, exponentially accurate asymptotic description of the exact Hermitian Yang--Mills metrics in the large K\"ahler limit. More precisely, the mean curvature of $H_{0,\epsilon}$ decays exponentially in every $C^k$-norm. Moreover, if $H_{1,\epsilon}$ denotes the exact Hermitian Yang--Mills metric and \[ H_\epsilon=H_{0,\epsilon}^{-1}H_{1,\epsilon}, \] then, after normalization, for every nonnegative integer $k$, there exist positive constants $C_k$ and $c_k$ such that \[ \|H_\epsilon- Id\|_{C^k}\leq C_k e^{-\frac{c_{k}}{\epsilon}}. \] The main analytic difficulty lies in the global $C^0$-comparison. Obtaining $C^0$-estimates for the coupled nonlinear Hermitian Yang--Mills system is intrinsically difficult; moreover, the equation controls only the contraction of the curvature, and hence only certain combinations of second derivatives, whereas one needs global control of the full matrix-valued metric.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29554
  11. [2608.29651] 关于$\mathbb P^3$和$S^2\times S^4$上复结构的注记
    Remarks on the Complex Structures on $\mathbb P^3$ and $S^2\times S^4$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文构造$\mathbb P^3$上的异种复结构。

    💡 研究单位切丛作为Cartan几何，证明其诱导几乎接触度量结构，并定义正规联络。
    📖 中文摘要

    Cartan几何是齐性空间的弯曲类似物，对应空间构造从一种类型的Cartan几何在更大的底流形上产生另一种类型的Cartan几何。黎曼流形的单位切丛在这种语言下是通过这种方式获得的类型$(\operatorname{Euc}(n),O(n-1))$的Cartan几何；我们一般地研究这种类型的Cartan几何，无论它们是否作为对应空间出现。我们证明每个这样的几何在其底流形上诱导一个几乎接触度量结构，以及切丛的正交分裂和相容线性联络；反之，这些数据决定几何，因此对应是双射。当$n\ge3$时，我们通过用Spencer微分表述的挠率条件挑选出一个独特的Cartan联络，我们称之为正规联络。这种规范化由对应空间构造决定：黎曼流形单位切丛上诱导的Cartan联络是正规的。

    🌐 English Abstract

We show that every such geometry induces on its base manifold an almost contact metric structure, together with an orthogonal splitting of the tangent bundle and a compatible linear connection; conversely, these data determine the geometry, so that the correspondence is a bijection. When $n\ge3$, we single out a distinguished Cartan connection, which we call normal, by a condition on its torsion formulated in terms of the Spencer differential. In this article, we develop some of its basic properties, building it from ground up. We will see that, aside from its homogeneous space description, the indefinite Grassmannian may be characterized in several other ways: set-theoretically, it is the manifold of indefinite $(p+q)$-dimensional subspaces in $(m +n)$-dimensional space; it is an adjoint orbit of a Lie group; a semialgebraic smooth manifold of matrices; a base space of a principal bundle whose total space is the indefinite Stiefel manifold, a natural corresponding notion. It may also be naturally equipped with various structures, turning the indefinite Grassmannian into a pseudo-Riemannian manifold; a Einstein manifold; a symplectic manifold; and a pseudo-K\"ahler manifold (last two only over $\mathbb{C}$). As a centerpiece of this article, we establish two attributes of the indefinite Grassmannian in relation to the standard Grassmannian: (i) any Grassmannian has a Whitney stratification whose highest dimensional strata are indefinite Grassmannians; (ii) the indefinite Grassmannian is a strong deformation retract of a product of two Grassmannians, thereby allowing us to completely ascertain the topology of the former. We will also determine some of the indefinite Grassmannian's features that are within reach --- both geometric (Riemann, Ricci, sectional, and scalar curvatures; second fundamental form) and topological (cohomology ring, homotopy groups, characteristic classes).

  22. [2608.30302] 扭曲凯勒-里奇流下局部阿诺德重数局部衰减公式的失效
    Failure of the Proposed Local Decay Formula for Local Arnold Multiplicities under Twisted K\"ahler--Ricci Flow
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究扭曲凯勒-里奇流下局部阿诺德重数的衰减公式。
    • 在 Hirzebruch 曲面上构造了反例，证明原公式不成立。
    • 发现衰减速度由残差类的 Zariski 负部分决定。
    • 在特定条件下证明了精确公式。
    • 结果对乘子理想和非局部性有重要推论。
    💡 构造了 Hirzebruch 曲面上的反例，表明扭曲凯勒-里奇流下局部阿诺德重数的衰减公式不成立。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 λ(u,x) 为拟多次调和函数 u 的局部阿诺德重数。Di Nezza-Guedj-Lu 曾问：扭曲凯勒-里奇流的每个极大弱解 φ_t 是否满足 λ(φ_t,x)=max{λ(φ_0,x)-t,0}。我们在 Hirzebruch 曲面 F_e=P_{P^1}(O_{P^1}⊕O_{P^1}(-e))（e≥2）上给出反例。设 S 为其负截面，F_1,...,F_k 为不同纤维。若 a,b_i>0，∑_i b_i>ea，且初始电流为 a[S]+∑_i b_i[F_i]，则对 x∈S\⋃_iF_i 和 0<t<min{a,b_1,...,b_k}，有 λ(φ_t,x)=a-min{k/e,1}t。因此当 k<e 时公式失效；较慢的衰减由残差类的 Zariski 负部分强制产生。在显式 SNC 和正性假设下，我们还证明了纯除子数据的精确曲面公式。推论包括固定背景数据下的非局部性和显式乘子理想。与光滑因子乘积给出每个复维数 n≥2 的反例。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\lambda(u,x)$ be the local Arnold multiplicity of a quasi-plurisubharmonic function $u$. Di Nezza--Guedj--Lu asked whether every maximal weak solution $\varphi_t$ of the twisted K\"ahler--Ricci flow satisfies $\lambda(\varphi_t,x)=\max\{\lambda(\varphi_0,x)-t,0\}$. We give counterexamples on the Hirzebruch surface $\mathbb F_e=\mathbb P_{\mathbb P^1} (\mathcal O_{\mathbb P^1}\oplus\mathcal O_{\mathbb P^1}(-e))$, $e\ge2$. Let $S$ be its negative section and $F_1,\ldots,F_k$ be distinct fibres. If $a,b_i>0$, $\sum_i b_i>ea$, and the initial current is $a[S]+\sum_i b_i[F_i]$, then $\lambda(\varphi_t,x)=a-\min\{k/e,1\}t$ for $x\in S\setminus\bigcup_iF_i$ and $0<t<\min\{a,b_1,\ldots,b_k\}$. Thus the formula fails for $k<e$; the slower decay is forced by the Zariski negative part of the residual class. Under explicit SNC and positivity hypotheses, we also prove an exact surface formula for pure divisorial data. Consequences include nonlocality with fixed background data and explicit multiplier ideals. Products with smooth factors give counterexamples in every complex dimension $n\ge2$.

    我们研究高维 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 空间的黎曼曲率张量的代数与微分结构，最终目标是为该度量族找到 IDEAL（内在、演绎、显式、算法）刻画。这些空间的特殊几何由主张量 h_{ab} 控制，这是一个非退化的闭共形 Killing-Yano 2-形式，其存在性单独挑出 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 族；因此我们专注于黎曼曲率 R_{abcd} 与 h_{ab} 之间的代数关系。在研究中我们遇到了一个障碍，导致一个 no-go 定理：没有非平凡的 2-形式（包括 h_{ab} 本身）可以仅由未微分的黎曼张量协变构造。尽管如此，我们确实通过代数条件和微分条件刻画了可能是 Kerr-NUT-(A)dS 度量曲率的黎曼对称张量族，这些条件源于 h_{ab} 定义方程的可积性和第二 Bianchi 恒等式。我们的计算适用于所有更高维度，这通过巧妙应用与稳定 h_{ab} 的半直积群 U(1)^n⋊S_n 相关的表示论技术而变得可行。

The special geometry of these spaces is governed by the principal tensor $h_{ab}$, a non-degenerate closed conformal Killing-Yano $2$-form, whose existence singles out the Kerr-NUT-(A)dS family; we therefore focus on the algebraic relationship between the Riemann curvature $R_{abcd}$ and $h_{ab}$. Our calculations apply in all higher dimensions, which becomes feasible by a judicious application of representation theoretic techniques related to a semi-direct product group $U(1)^n\rtimes S_n$ that stabilizes $h_{ab}$.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文建立 Steklov 特征值的等直径型上界。
    • 第一个估计线性依赖 k 且最优。
    • 第二个估计二次依赖 k 且最优。
    • 回答了开放问题。
    💡 建立了有界 Lipschitz 域上 Steklov 特征值的等直径型上界，回答了开放问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文建立了有界 Lipschitz 域 Ω⊂R^n（n≥2）上 Steklov 特征值的等直径型上界，对所有 k∈N 成立：D(Ω)σ_k(Ω)≤C(n)k，D(Ω)σ_k(Ω)≤C(n)k^2(|Ω|^{1/n}/D(Ω))^{n/(n-1)}，其中 C(n)>0 仅依赖于 n。第一个估计在 k 的线性依赖上是最优的，而第二个在体积-直径比的指数和（一旦该指数固定）k 的二次依赖上都是最优的。特别地，第二个估计肯定回答了 [5] 中的开放问题 4.37。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we establish isodiametric-type upper bounds for Steklov eigenvalues of bounded Lipschitz domains $\Omega\subset \mathbb{R}^n$, $n\geq 2$, valid for every $k\in \mathbb{N}$: \[ D(\Omega)\sigma_k(\Omega)\leq C(n)k, \] \[ D(\Omega)\sigma_k(\Omega)\leq C(n)k^2\left(\frac{|\Omega|^{\frac{1}{n}}}{D(\Omega)}\right)^{\frac{n}{n-1}}, \] where $C(n)>0$ depends only on $n$. The first estimate is sharp in its linear dependence on $k$, while the second is sharp both in the exponent of the volume-diameter ratio and, once that exponent is fixed, in its quadratic dependence on $k$. In particular, the second estimate gives an affirmative answer to Open Question 4.37 in [5].

    • 本文研究 ALF Ricci 平坦 4-流形的线性稳定性。
    • 证明了有界 Killing 场时稳定当且仅当局部超凯勒。
    • 证明了 Li-Sun 度量的不稳定性。
    • 高维中稳定性迫使万有覆盖分裂线。
    💡 证明了具有有界 Killing 矢量场的完备 ALF Ricci 平坦 4-流形线性稳定当且仅当局部超凯勒，并推广到高维。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明具有有界 Killing 矢量场的完备 ALF Ricci 平坦 4-流形是线性稳定的当且仅当它是局部超凯勒的。这统一适用于所有已知例子，特别证明了 Li-Sun 最近发现的所有度量的不稳定性。论证基于与 Killing 场相关的 Ricci 平坦度量的无穷小 Einstein-Maxwell 形变。一个独立的更简单恒等式证明了非平坦静态 Ricci 平坦 4-度量的不稳定性，包括静态轴对称光滑黎曼 Myers/Korotkin-Nicolai 度量。据我们所知，这证明了所有已知 Ricci 平坦 4-流形族中线性稳定性与特殊全纯和（反）自对偶的等价性。在更高维中，对于 AF 流形的推广，我们证明稳定性迫使万有覆盖分裂一条线。这给出了所有维数中黎曼 Myers-Perry 瞬子和黎曼 Schwarzschild-Tangherlini 度量的显式失稳张量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that a complete ALF Ricci-flat 4-manifold carrying a bounded Killing vector field is linearly stable if and only if it is locally hyperk\"ahler. This applies uniformly to all known examples and in particular proves the instability of all of the metrics recently found by Li-Sun. The argument is based on infinitesimal Einstein-Maxwell deformations of the Ricci-flat metric associated with the Killing field. A separate simpler identity proves the instability of nonflat static Ricci-flat 4-metrics which include the static axisymmetric smooth Riemannian Myers/Korotkin-Nicolai metrics. To our knowledge, this proves the equivalence between linear stability and special holonomy and (anti-)selfduality for all known families of Ricci-flat 4-manifolds. In higher dimensions, for generalizations of AF manifolds, we show that stability forces the universal cover to split a line. This gives explicit destabilizing tensors on the Riemannian Myers-Perry instantons and the Riemannian Schwarzschild-Tangherlini metrics in all dimensions.

    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that when the J-equation does not admit a smooth solution, there is always only a finite number of obstructing optimally destabilizing curves and divisors on compact K\"ahler manifolds of any dimension. For threefolds, this establishes the unconditional finiteness of all optimally destabilizing subvarieties. We moreover characterize the union of these optimal subvarieties, proving that they are contained in the locus where a natural continuity path loses local smooth compactness. Finally, to address subvarieties that are neither curves nor divisors, we prove a rigidity criterion which prevents optimal cycles from moving in compact one-parameter families, with further consequences for their deformation and symmetry properties.

  37. [2608.31145] 统计流形上的规范相容张量：分裂与子流形几何
    Gauge-compatible tensors on statistical manifolds: splitting and submanifold geometry
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究统计流形上的规范相容张量。
    • 用统计差张量刻画了规范方程。
    • 证明了 Θ 交织平行移动，秩恒定。
    • 在正交分解下得到局部乘积结构。
    • 推导了子流形的切向和法向方程。
    💡 研究了满足规范方程的 (1,1)-张量场，给出了局部乘积分解和子流形几何。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究统计流形 (M,g,∇,∇*) 上带有非零 (1,1)-张量场 Θ 且满足规范方程 ∇_X(ΘY)=Θ(∇_X*Y) 的结构。我们首先用统计差张量 K=∇-∇^g 刻画此条件。当 Θ 关于 Levi-Civita 联络平行时，规范方程等价于 K_XΘ=-ΘK_X。我们进一步证明 Θ 交织对偶联络的平行移动。因此，其秩在每个连通分量上恒定，且 kerΘ 和 ImΘ 确定光滑可积分布。如果此外 TM=kerΘ⊥⊕ImΘ，我们建立统计结构的局部乘积分解。然后我们研究带有 Θ-不变和 Θ-反不变分布的的子流形，并推导环境规范方程在第二基本形式和形状算子下的切向和法向分量。我们还获得曲率交织推论，并给出一个非全测地例子说明子流形恒等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study statistical manifolds $(M,g,\nabla,\nabla^{*})$ endowed with a nonzero $(1,1)$-tensor field $\Theta$ satisfying the gauge equation \[ \nabla_X(\Theta Y)=\Theta(\nabla_X^{*}Y). \] We first characterize this condition in terms of the statistical difference tensor \[ K=\nabla-\nabla^{g}. \] When $\Theta$ is parallel with respect to the Levi-Civita connection, the gauge equation is equivalent to \[ K_X\Theta=-\Theta K_X. \] We further show that $\Theta$ intertwines the parallel transports of the dual connections. Consequently, its rank is constant on every connected component, and $\ker\Theta$ and $\operatorname{Im}\Theta$ determine smooth integrable distributions. If, in addition, \[ TM=\ker\Theta\overset{\perp}{\oplus}\operatorname{Im}\Theta, \] we establish a local product decomposition of the statistical structure. We then study submanifolds carrying $\Theta$-invariant and $\Theta$-anti-invariant distributions and derive the tangential and normal components of the ambient gauge equation in terms of the second fundamental forms and shape operators of the dual statistical connections. We also obtain curvature-intertwining consequences and present a non-totally-geodesic example illustrating the submanifold identities.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31145

    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了任意维数和余维数的 varifold 关于各向异性能量的 Michael-Simon 不等式。结合 Allard 的各向异性正则性定理，该结果给出所有维数中具有有界各向异性平均曲率的超曲面的正则性。使用 De Philippis 和 Pigati 的结果，它还暗示具有一致有界各向异性第一变差的可求积 varifold 的密度界和紧性性质。证明依赖于各向异性应力测度的投影方法以及 Gennaioli 和 Rindler 最近解决的消失质量猜想。

Combined with Allard's anisotropic regularity theorem, this result yields the regularity of hypersurfaces with bounded anisotropic mean curvature in every dimension. Using the results of De Philippis and Pigati, it also implies density bounds and compactness properties for rectifiable varifolds with uniformly bounded anisotropic first variation. The proof relies on a projection method for anisotropic stress measures as well as the recent resolution of the vanishing mass conjecture by Gennaioli and Rindler.

  41. [2608.28881] 非线性Li-Yau不等式及其推论
    A nonlinear Li-Yau inequality and its consequences
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究规范化p-拉普拉斯热方程正解的非线性Li-Yau不等式。
    • 在非负Ricci曲率闭流形上证明尖锐不等式，常数最优。
    • 证明基于逼近流的Bochner型恒等式，避免临界集假设。
    • 不等式在Ricci下界和完备非紧情形也成立。
    • 作为推论得到全局Harnack不等式。
    💡 证明规范化p-拉普拉斯热方程正解的非线性Li-Yau不等式，常数最优，并推导全局Harnack不等式。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了在具有非负Ricci曲率的闭黎曼流形上，规范化抛物型p-拉普拉斯方程 $u_t = \Delta u + (p-2)|\nabla u|^{-2}\nabla^2u(\nabla u,\nabla u)$（$1<p<\infty$）的正解满足一个尖锐的非线性Li-Yau不等式，该方程在解的临界集上按粘性意义解释。常数在闭流形类中也是最优的：它由平坦空间$\mathbb R^n$中的显式自相似解恒等达到，其尖锐性通过平坦环面$\mathbb R^n/(L\mathbb Z)^n$（$L \to \infty$）的大环面极限传递到紧致情形。证明依赖于一族一致抛物逼近流的精确Bochner型恒等式，其中二阶正则化和一阶eikonal项解耦：对于该族，极大值原理以尖锐常数适用，一致于正则化参数，且对解的临界集无假设。该恒等式在Li-Yau泛函的经典$\alpha$-松弛下形式不变；因此我们还在$\operatorname{Ric} \ge -\kappa$的闭流形上（同样仅需Ricci下界）获得相应不等式，并在$\operatorname{Ric} \ge 0$的完备非紧流形上，在距离函数的截断假设（在$\mathbb R^n$中自动成立，且对于$p \ge 2$在非负截面曲率下成立）和Li-Yau量的定性多项式增长条件（极值剖面以指数零满足）下，获得逼近流的尖锐不等式。由此推出一个尖锐的全局Harnack不等式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a sharp nonlinear version of the celebrated Li-Yau inequality for positive solutions of the normalized parabolic $p$-Laplacian equation $u_t = \Delta u + (p-2)|\nabla u|^{-2}\nabla^2u(\nabla u,\nabla u)$, $1<p<\infty$, on a closed Riemannian manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature, the equation being interpreted in the viscosity sense on the critical set of the solution. The constant is best possible, even within the class of closed manifolds: it is attained identically by an explicit self-similar solution in flat $\Rn$, and its sharpness transfers to the compact setting through a large-torus limit along the flat tori $\mathbb R^n/(L\mathbb Z)^n$, $L \to \infty$. The proof rests on an exact Bochner-type identity for a family of uniformly parabolic approximating flows in which the second-order regularization and the first-order eikonal term are decoupled: for this family the maximum principle applies with the sharp constant, uniformly in the regularization parameter, and with no assumption on the critical set of the solution. The identity is moreover form-invariant under the classical $\alpha$-relaxation of the Li-Yau functional; as a consequence we also obtain the corresponding inequality on closed manifolds with $\operatorname{Ric} \ge -\kappa$ (again with no assumption beyond the Ricci lower bound), and, on complete noncompact manifolds with $\operatorname{Ric} \ge 0$, the sharp inequality for the approximating flows -- again with no assumption on the critical set -- under a cutoff hypothesis on the distance function (automatic in $\Rn$, and, for $p \ge 2$, under nonnegative sectional curvature) and a qualitative polynomial growth condition on the Li-Yau quantity, satisfied, with exponent zero, by the extremal profile. A sharp global Harnack inequality follows.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28881

These results provide a rigorous extension of the quadrature surface free boundary framework from the Euclidean setting to compact Riemannian manifolds.

    我们研究希尔伯特空间$H$中闭稠定算子图的集合$\Gamma$，将其视为$H\times H$中正交投影的Grassmann流形$P(H\times H)$的子集。我们证明有界算子图的子集$\Gamma^b$是以零算子图$P_0$（投影到$H\times\{0\}$）为中心的$P(H\times H)$的开单位球。该球通过映射$T\mapsto P_T$（$T$的图${Gr(T)}$上的投影）与$B(H)$微分同胚。我们证明无界闭算子的图位于$\Gamma$的边界。我们还研究了连接两个图$Gr(A)$、$Gr(B)$的$P(H\times H)$的最小测地线的存在性和特征。如果$A,B$是自伴的，这样的测地线总是存在，我们利用与子空间对$Gr(A)$、$Gr(B)$关联的五空间分解显式构造了一个特殊的指数。一个显式的低维例子表明，连接两个图的测地线不必保持在$\Gamma$内，即不完全由图组成。我们还将紧算子的图与受限Grassmann流形联系起来，并研究对$Gr(S)$、$Gr(T)$的公共补问题，在一个算子有界或在下有界条件下给出正结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the set $\Gamma$ of graphs of closed, densely defined operators in a Hilbert space $H$, regarded as a subset of the Grassmann manifold $P(H\times H)$ of orthogonal projections in $H\times H$. We show that the subset $\Gamma^b$ of graphs of bounded operators is the open unit ball of $P(H\times H)$ centered at the graph of the zero operator $P_0$ (which projects onto $H\times\{0\}$). This ball is diffeomorphic to $B(H)$ via the map $T\mapsto P_T$ ($=$ the projection onto the graph ${Gr(T)}$ of $T$). We show that graphs of unbounded closed operators lie at the boundary of $\Gamma$. We also study the existence and characteristics of minimal geodesics of $P(H\times H)$ joining two graphs $Gr(A)$, $Gr(B)$. If $A,B$ are selfadjoint, such a geodesic always exists, and we construct explicitly a distinguished exponent using the five-space decomposition associated to the pair of subspaces $Gr(A)$, $Gr(B)$. An explicit low-dimensional example shows that the geodesic joining two graphs need not remain inside $\Gamma$, i.e., does not consist entirely of graphs. We also relate graphs of compact operators to the restricted Grassmannian, and study the problem of common complements for pairs $Gr(S)$, $Gr(T)$, giving positive results when one operator is bounded or under lower boundedness conditions.

  47. [2608.30174] 矩形环面上带一个锥奇点的球面度量的精确计数
    Exact counting of spherical metrics with one conical singularity on rectangular tori
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究矩形环面上奇异Liouville方程的解计数。
    • 证明在给定范围内恰好有n个轴对称解。
    • 结合先前结果，得出球面度量存在性的精确计数。
    • 方法基于线性化方程平凡解和Lamé方程单值性。
    💡 证明矩形环面上奇异Liouville方程在给定范围内恰好有n个解，从而计数球面度量。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明对于每个整数$n\geq 2$和$8\pi(n-1)<\rho<8\pi n$，矩形环面$E_{\mathrm{i}b}=\mathbb{C}/(\mathbb Z+\mathrm{i} b\mathbb Z)$上的奇异Liouville方程$\Delta u+\e^u=\rho\delta_0$恰好有$n$个解，且所有解都是轴对称的。结合Chen-Lin和Lin-Wang的先前结果，这得出：\begin{itemize} \item 只要$\vartheta$是正奇数，$E_{\mathrm{i} b}$就没有带锥角$2\pi\vartheta$的球面度量。\item 对于每个整数$n\geq 1$，$E_{\mathrm{i} b}$对每个$\vartheta\in (2n-1, 2n+1)$恰好有$n$个带锥角$2\pi\vartheta$的球面度量。\end{itemize} 基本思想是证明线性化方程在轴对称函数空间中只有平凡解。先前通过Bol等周不等式分析节点域的方法仅适用于$\rho\leq 8\pi$。我们通过探索与经典Lamé方程单值性的深层联系，为所有$\rho\in (0,+\infty)\setminus 8\pi\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}$开发了一种统一方法。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that for every integer $n\geq 2$ and $8\pi(n-1)<\rho<8\pi n$, the singular Liouville equation $\Delta u+\e^u=\rho\delta_0$ on a rectangular torus $E_{\mathrm{i}b}=\mathbb{C}/(\mathbb Z+\mathrm{i} b\mathbb Z)$ has exactly $n$ solutions, which are all axisymmetric. Together with previous results by Chen-Lin and Lin-Wang, this yields that \begin{itemize} \item $E_{\mathrm{i} b}$ admits no spherical metrics with a conical singularity of angle $2\pi\vartheta$ as long as $\vartheta$ is a positive odd integer. \item For every integer $n\geq 1$, $E_{\mathrm{i} b}$ admits exactly $n$ spherical metrics with a conical singularity of angle $2\pi\vartheta$ for each $\vartheta\in (2n-1, 2n+1)$. \end{itemize} The basic idea is to prove that the linearized equation has only trivial solutions in the space of axisymmetric functions. The previous method of analysing nodal domains via Bol's isoperimetric inequality only works for $\rho\leq 8\pi$. We develop a unified approach for all $\rho\in (0,+\infty)\setminus 8\pi\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}$ by exploring the deep connection with the monodromy of the classical Lam\'{e} equation.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们基于过滤微分分次代数（FDGA）的Rees构造发展了一个纯代数的指标型定理框架。除了经典的Rees模，我们引入了一个光滑变体$C^\omega_{\mathcal{R}}A$，适用于解析论证，并研究了迹及其到Rees代数的逐点和系数扩展。主要结果（推论3.11）是一个抽象指标定理：给定兼容的过滤微分分次结合代数与迹$A$、$B$、$G$，以及态射$\phi\colon A\to B$、作用$\rho\colon G\otimes A\to A$和态射$i\colon G\to B$，以及一个分次中心元素，有$\mathrm{tr}_B(e^{f_0+f_1}) = \mathrm{tr}_{\widehat{\mathrm{gr}}A}(e^{\gamma}h)$，其中左边是$B$中的迹，右边是$A$的Laurent级数相关分次$\widehat{\mathrm{gr}}A$中的迹。这里$f_0+f_1$是曲率型元素，即形式为$d_B\beta+\beta^2$的元素（$\beta$为奇次元素），而$\gamma$和$h$是$\widehat{\mathrm{gr}}A$中的某些元素。该形式体系以Getzler重标度技术和Ludewig-Yi对环空间Chern特征的局部化公式推导为模型。

The main result (Corollary 3.11) is an abstract index theorem: given a compatible datum of filtered differential graded associative algebras with traces $A$, $B$, $G$ with a morphism $\phi\colon A\to B$, an action $\rho\colon G\otimes A\to A$ and a morphism $i\colon G\to B$, together with a graded-central element, one has $\mathrm{tr}_B(e^{f_0+f_1}) = \mathrm{tr}_{\widehat{\mathrm{gr}}A}(e^{\gamma}h)$, where the left-hand side is the trace in $B$ and the right-hand side the trace in the Laurent series associated graded $\widehat{\mathrm{gr}}A$ of $A$. Here $f_0+f_1$ is a curvature-type element, i.e., an element of the form $d_B\beta+\beta^2$ for some odd-degree element $\beta$, while $\gamma$ and $h$ are certain elements in $\widehat{\mathrm{gr}}A$. The formalism is modelled on the Getzler rescaling technique and on the derivation of the localization formula for the loop space Chern character by Ludewig and Yi.

  49. [2608.30625] 通过Bernstein技巧重新审视四阶Hénon方程的Fazly-Wei-Xu点态估计
    Revisiting Fazly-Wei-Xu pointwise estimates for the fourth-order H\'enon equation via Bernstein's technique
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 重新证明四阶Hénon方程的点态估计。
    • 使用Bernstein技巧和极大值原理，替代Moser迭代。
    • 放宽有界性条件，估计对任意sigma成立。
    • 证明系数最优，回答原论文问题。
    • 推论：存在正曲率共形度量。
    💡 用Bernstein技巧给出四阶Hénon方程点态估计的新证明，放宽条件并证明系数最优。
    📖 中文摘要

    在M. Fazly、J. Wei和X. Xu发表于Analysis and PDE 8 (2015) 1541-1563的杰出工作中，他们建立了以下点态估计：\[-\Delta u \geq \frac 2{n-4} \frac{|\nabla u|^2}u + \sqrt{\frac 2{p+1- \frac 8{n(n-4)}}} |x|^{\frac \sigma 2} u^\frac{p+1}2 \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] 对于四阶Hénon方程\[\Delta^2 u = |x|^\sigma u^p \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\]的任何有界、正、$C^4$解$u$，其中$n \geq 5$，$\sigma \geq 0 $但隐含地接近0，且$p>(n+4+2\sigma)/(n-4)$。他们的论证依赖于标准Moser证明风格的复杂迭代论证。在本文中，我们提供了一种替代方法，依赖于通过极大值原理的Bernstein技巧。我们论证的核心是适当选择辅助函数，使我们不仅能达到相同的点态估计（该估计甚至对$p = (n+4+2\sigma)/(n-4)$也成立），而且放宽了解的有界性。此外，点态不等式实际上对任何$\sigma \geq 0$都成立。这个估计导致了一个有趣的推论：$\mathbf R^n$上存在具有正标量曲率和$Q$曲率的共形度量。我们还证明了系数$2/(n-4)$是最优的，从而首次回答了上述论文中的问题。我们的证明似乎更简单且具有构造性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In a remarkable work published in Analysis and PDE 8 (2015) 1541-1563, M. Fazly, J. Wei, and X. Xu establish the following pointwise estimate \[-\Delta u \geq \frac 2{n-4} \frac{|\nabla u|^2}u + \sqrt{\frac 2{p+1- \frac 8{n(n-4)}}} |x|^{\frac \sigma 2} u^\frac{p+1}2 \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] for any bounded, positive, $C^4$-solution $u$ to the the fourth-order H\'enon equation \[\Delta^2 u = |x|^\sigma u^p \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] with $n \geq 5$, $\sigma \geq 0 $ but implicitly near $0$, and $p>(n+4+2\sigma)/(n-4)$. Their argument relies on a sophisticated iteration argument in the fashion of the standard Moser proof. In this paper, we provide an alternative approach which relies on the Bernstein technique via the maximum principle. The heart of our argument is a suitable choice of an auxiliary function allowing us not only achieving the same pointwise estimate, which still holds even for $p = (n+4+2\sigma)/(n-4)$, but also relaxing the boundedness of solutions. In addition, the pointwise inequality actually holds for any $\sigma \geq 0$. This estimate leads to an interesting consequence of the existence of conformal metric of $\mathbf R^n$ having positive scalar and $Q$ curvatures. We also show that the coefficient $2/(n-4)$ is sharp, hence providing the first answer to the question in the op. cit. paper. Our proof appears to be simpler and constructive.

    💡 研究紧李群上次拉普拉斯的热核、次黎曼距离和谱zeta函数，给出展开和行列式分解。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究了由紧李群的闭连通子群确定的次拉普拉斯的热核、次黎曼距离和谱zeta函数。将Hall的反演公式与紧群热核的仿射格展开相结合，我们推导出涉及群指数格的Cartan积分表示。对于两步紧李对，小时间热迹展开的完整代数形式由两个显式常数$C_{G,L}$和$\beta_{G,L}$确定，直到指数小余项。该展开确定了所有热系数、约化谱zeta函数的极点和留数，以及其在非正整数处的值。对于平移对称子类，我们证明了Carnot-Carathéodory距离的均匀垂直渐近；前导系数$\mathfrak F_{G,K}(Z)$是有限维奇异值问题的达到最小值。对于单连通两步对，我们获得了zeta正则化行列式分解为局部、格和谱项的精确分解，并带有指数截断估计。我们将这些结果专门应用于$\mathrm{SU}(N)$的块子群，恢复了经典$\mathrm{SU}(2)$和CR球面谱。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study heat kernels, sub-Riemannian distances, and spectral zeta functions of sub-Laplacians determined by closed connected subgroups of compact Lie groups. Combining Hall's inversion formula with the affine lattice expansion of the compact group heat kernel, we derive a Cartan integral representation involving the group's exponential lattice. For two-step compact Lie pairs, the full algebraic small-time heat trace expansion is determined, up to an exponentially small remainder, by two explicit constants $C_{G,L}$ and $\beta_{G,L}$. This expansion determines all heat coefficients, the poles and residues of the reduced spectral zeta function, and its values at nonpositive integers. For the transvective symmetric subclass, we prove uniform vertical asymptotics for the Carnot-Carath\'eodory distance; the leading coefficient $\mathfrak F_{G,K}(Z)$ is the attained minimum of a finite-dimensional singular value problem. For simply connected two-step pairs, we obtain an exact decomposition of the zeta-regularized determinant into local, lattice, and spectral terms, with exponential truncation estimates. We specialize these results to block subgroups of $\mathrm{SU}(N)$, recovering the classical $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ and CR sphere spectra.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文提供了两种算法从样本中提取动力系统的控制方程，即\acf{KR}和\acf{IKEA}。这些算法提取一组功能独立的Koopman特征函数。这种集合信息量最大，因为它揭示了动力系统的几何结构。因此，尽管基数有限，控制方程被完美地恢复。因此，实现了简约原则。样本可以从向量场本身或系统的轨道中获取。算法\acl{KR}从向量场的样本中提取控制律，算法\acl{IKEA}从系统轨迹的样本中提取。这两种算法都是基于约束优化的方法，用于从稀疏和损坏的向量场或轨迹样本中学习控制方程。基于Koopman偏微分方程的目标泛函，依靠样本逼近Koopman特征函数，可行泛函迫使该集合功能独立。功能独立的条件在这里基于Gershgorin圆定理进行了深入讨论和表述。对于两种算法，优化过程由带回溯的罚方法诱导，在去噪、泛化和降维方面产生了有希望的结果，并在动力系统恢复方面显示出比\acs{DMD}、扩展\acs{DMD}、\acs{PINN}和\acs{SINDy}更好的结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    This paper provides two algorithms to extract the governing equations of the dynamical system from samples, \acf{KR} and \acf{IKEA}. These algorithms extract a functionally independent set of Koopman Eigenfunctions. This kind of set is the most informative since it reveals the geometric structure of the dynamical system. Consequently, despite its finite cardinality, the governing equations are immaculately restored. Thus, the principle of parsimony is implemented. Samples can be taken either from a vector field itself or from orbits of the system. The algorithm \acl{KR} extracts the governing law from samples of the vector field, and the algorithm \acl{IKEA} extracts it from samples of trajectories of the system. Both of these algorithms are constrained optimization-based methods for learning the governing equations from sparse and corrupted samples of the vector field or trajectories. The objective functional, based on the Koopman Partial Differential Equation, approximates the Koopman Eigenfunctions relying on the samples, and the feasible functional forces this set to be functionally independent. The condition for functional independence is thoroughly discussed and formulated here, based on the Gershgorin Circle Theorem. For both algorithms, the optimization process is induced by the penalty method with backtracking that yields promising results in denoising, generalization, and dimensionality reduction, and shows better results than \acs{DMD}, Extended \acs{DMD}, \acs{PINN}, and \acs{SINDy} in dynamical system restoration.

As an application, when the right-hand side is identically $1$, we prove that every entire admissible solution in $\mathbb{R}^n$ with quadratic growth is necessarily a quadratic polynomial.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究双曲曲面序列上薛定谔特征函数的量子混合。
    • 在Benjamini-Schramm极限下证明混合性质。
    • 适用于多项式混合可观测量。
    • 证明结合测地流混合和波核估计。
    💡 证明一致离散且Benjamini-Schramm收敛到双曲平面的紧致双曲曲面序列上薛定谔特征函数的量子混合。
    📖 中文摘要

    设$\{X_n\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}$为一致离散且Benjamini-Schramm收敛到$\mathbb{H}$的紧致双曲曲面序列，设$\{V_n\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}$为势序列，使得$V_n$的$L^2$-范数相对于$X_n$的体积为$o(1)$。我们证明了$ -\Delta_{X_n}+V_n$的特征函数在任何足够大的谱窗口$I$中对有界可观测量的量子混合，这些可观测量关于测地流是多项式混合的。此类势序列的例子包括刚好低于热力学极限的点云势、双曲空间中稀薄玻色气体的Hartree势，以及由$L^p(\mathbb{H})$（某个$p>0$）中的有界势诱导的序列。这是此类结果首次超越局部对称设置。证明结合了$T^1X_n$上的经典测地流混合（取代Nevo遍历定理）、Duhamel公式以及第一作者最近发展的几何波核估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\{X_n\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}$ be a sequence of compact hyperbolic surfaces which is uniformly discrete and Benjamini-Schramm converges to $\mathbb{H}$ and let $\{V_n\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}$ be a sequence of potentials such that the $L^2$-norm of $V_n$ is $o(1)$ with respect to the volume of $X_n$. We prove quantum mixing for the eigenfunctions of $-\Delta_{X_n}+V_n$ in any sufficiently large spectral window $I$ for bounded observables which are polynomial mixing with respect to the geodesic flow. Examples of such sequences of potentials include point-cloud potentials just below the thermodynamic limit, Hartree potentials for dilute Bose gases in hyperbolic space, and sequences induced by bounded potentials in $L^p(\mathbb{H})$ for some $p>0$. This is the first result of this kind beyond the locally symmetric setting. The proof combines classical geodesic flow mixing on $T^1X_n$, replacing Nevo's ergodic theorem, with the Duhamel formula and recently developed geometric wave-kernel estimates by the first author.

    Cultural Bias Without a Cultural Self:A Disassociation Study of LLM's Persona and Bias
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究LLM文化人格分离的本质。
    • 提出用相关矩阵流形区分特质与偏见。
    • 人类数据支持特质存在，LLM数据不支持。
    • LLM的分离源于共享项目顺序的模板。
    • 文化信号是群体模板而非个体特质。
    💡 证明大语言模型的文化人格分离是表面模板而非内在特质，并给出区分特质与偏见的准则。
    📖 中文摘要

    在跨文化研究中，带有文化人格提示的语言模型越来越多地代替人类受访者。它们的回答能清晰地区分人格，这种分离被视为文化观点的证据。我们表明分离是真实的，但观点并非如此，并且有一个标准可以区分它们。特质是单个受访者内部的结构，在测量框架改变后仍然存在；偏见只需要特定群体的项目均值。为了检验前者，我们将单个响应集表示为项目-维度矩阵，并将其相关矩阵视为对称正定矩阵流形上的一个点。在人类中，这承载了特质应有的东西：它在没有共享项目、顺序或上下文的测试-重测会话中重现（r=0.77，N=89）；在公开的NEO-PI-R数据上，它以76%的识别率识别个体（随机水平0.4%，N=263）；并预测GPA（R²=0.281，p=0.003），而来自相同响应的BigFive聚合预测不到任何东西（R²=0.018）。在四个前沿LLM中，它什么也返回不了。人格结构只有在每个实例共享一个项目顺序时才可读：给每个实例自己的顺序，分离率从94.7%下降到随机水平，而将实例重新对齐到任何共享的随机顺序则恢复到82-84%。独立逐项生成且没有潜在结构的响应重现了整个模式。文化信号是一个群体模板，而不是任何实例的属性，对齐机制的不同只在于哪种刻板印象在表面上存活。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Language models prompted with cultural personas increasingly stand in for human respondents in cross-cultural research. Their responses separate personas cleanly, and that separation is read as evidence of a cultural point of view. We show that the separation is real, that the point of view is not, and that one criterion tells them apart. A trait is structure internal to one respondent that survives a change of measurement frame; a bias needs only group-specific item means. To test for the first, we represent a single response set as an Item--Dimension matrix and treat its correlation matrix as a point on the manifold of symmetric positive definite matrices. In humans this carries what a trait should: it reproduces across test--retest sessions sharing no items, order or context ($r=0.77$, $N=89$); on public NEO-PI-R data it identifies individuals at up to $76\%$ against a $0.4\%$ chance level ($N=263$); and it predicts GPA ($R^2=0.281$, $p=0.003$) where BigFive aggregates from the same responses predict nothing ($R^2=0.018$). In four frontier LLMs it returns nothing. Persona structure is readable only while every instance shares one item order: give each its own order and separation falls from $94.7\%$ to chance, while realigning instances to \emph{any} shared random order restores it to $82$--$84\%$. Responses generated independently item by item, with no latent structure, reproduce the entire pattern. The cultural signal is a group template, not a property of any instance, and alignment regimes differ only in which stereotype survives on the surface.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368

    📖 中文摘要

    Banach在1932年问：一个实Banach空间X，如果对于某个固定的1<n<dim X，其所有n维子空间都线性等距，那么X是否必须是Hilbert空间？Gromov证明了偶数n的情况，后续工作解决了一些奇数维情况。我们证明了所有奇数n的情况，包括所有先前未解决的情况。连同Gromov的偶数n结果，这完成了实情形的Banach等距猜想。证明结合了主丛理论和Brouwer度论证。

Together with Gromov's result for even $n$, this completes Banach's isometric conjecture in the real case. The proof combines principal bundle theory with a Brouwer degree argument.

    Small separators, upper bounds for $l^\infty$-widths, and systolic geometry
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究闭子流形的$l^\infty$-宽度与体积关系。
    • 证明宽度上界，维数依赖$\sqrt{n}$，余维1时改进。
    • 应用于脉动几何，得到非收缩闭曲线存在性。
    • 引入非线性Federer-Fleming论证和运动学公式。
    💡 证明闭子流形的$l^\infty$-宽度与体积的不等式，维数依赖为$\sqrt{n}$，并应用于脉动几何。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究闭子流形$M^n\subset \mathbb{R}^N$的欧氏体积与其$l^\infty$-宽度$W^{l^\infty}_{n-1}(M^n)$（定义为所有连续映射$\phi:M^n\longrightarrow K^{n-1}\subset\mathbb{R}^N$的$\sup_{x\in M^n}\Vert \phi(x)-x\Vert_{l^\infty}$的下确界）之间的不等式对维数的依赖。我们证明$W^{l^\infty}_{n-1}(M^n)\leq const\ \sqrt{n}\ vol(M^n)^{\frac{1}{n}}$，如果余维$N-n$等于$1$，则$W^{l^\infty}_{n-1}(M^n)\leq \sqrt{3}\ vol(M^n)^{\frac{1}{n}}$。作为推论，我们证明如果$M^n\subset \mathbb{R}^N$是{\it本质的}，则$M^n$上存在一条非收缩闭曲线包含在$\mathbb{R}^N$中边长为$const\ \sqrt{n}\ vol^{\frac{1}{n}}(M^n)$且边平行于坐标轴的立方体内。如果余维为$1$，则立方体的边长为$4\ vol^{\frac{1}{n}}(M^n)$。为了证明这些结果，我们引入了一种新的脉动几何方法，可描述为经典Federer-Fleming论证的非线性版本，其中我们从$\mathbb{R}^N$中一个特别构造的非线性$(N-n)$维复形推出，该复形不与$M^n$相交。为了构造这些复形，我们首先证明运动学公式的一个版本，其中对$l^N_\infty$的等距取平均（定理3.5），并引入[KORW]和[AK]中最近发现的最优泡沫的高余维类似物。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We investigate the dependence on the dimension in the inequalities that relate the Euclidean volume of a closed submanifold $M^n\subset \mathbb{R}^N$ with its $l^\infty$-width $W^{l^\infty}_{n-1}(M^n)$ defined as the infimum over all continuous maps $\phi:M^n\longrightarrow K^{n-1}\subset\mathbb{R}^N$ of $sup_{x\in M^n}\Vert \phi(x)-x\Vert_{l^\infty}$. We prove that $W^{l^\infty}_{n-1}(M^n)\leq const\ \sqrt{n}\ vol(M^n)^{\frac{1}{n}}$, and if the codimension $N-n$ is equal to $1$, then $W^{l^\infty}_{n-1}(M^n)\leq \sqrt{3}\ vol(M^n)^{\frac{1}{n}}$. As a corollary, we prove that if $M^n\subset \mathbb{R}^N$ is {\it essential}, then there exists a non-contractible closed curve on $M^n$ contained in a cube in $\mathbb{R}^N$ with side length $const\ \sqrt{n}\ vol^{\frac{1}{n}}(M^n)$ with sides parallel to the coordinate axes. If the codimension is $1$, then the side length of the cube is $4\ vol^{\frac{1}{n}}(M^n)$. To prove these results we introduce a new approach to systolic geometry that can be described as a non-linear version of the classical Federer-Fleming argument, where we push out from a specially constructed non-linear $(N-n)$-dimensional complex in $\mathbb{R}^N$ that does not intersect $M^n$. To construct these complexes we first prove a version of kinematic formula where one averages over isometries of $l^N_\infty$ (Theorem 3.5), and introduce high-codimension analogs of optimal foams recently discovered in [KORW] and [AK].

    • 构造了非弱λ-集的γ-集。
    • 得到B1的Baire性质差异。
    • 回答了先前提出的问题。
    💡 在ZFC中构造了可分的伪紧弱λ-空间非Δ1-空间，并构造了非弱λ-集的γ-集，回答了先前问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文证明了（在ZFC中）存在一个可分的伪紧弱λ-空间X，它不是Δ1-空间。由此得出存在空间X使得B1(X,[0,1])是Choquet的，而B1(X)不是Choquet的。还证明了存在一个γ-集X不是弱λ-集。由此得出存在零维可分度量空间X使得B1(X)是Baire的，而B1(X,[0,1])不是Choquet的。这些结果回答了先前提出的问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper it is proved that there exists (in $ZFC$) a separable pseudocompact weak $\lambda$-space $X$ which is not $\Delta_1$-space. It follows that there exists a space $X$ such that $B_1(X,[0, 1])$ is Choquet, while $B_1(X)$ is not Choquet. Also it is proved that there exists a $\gamma$-set $X$ which is not weak $\lambda$-set. It follows that there exists a zero-dimensional separable metrizable space $X$ such that $B_1(X)$ is Baire, while $B_1(X, [0,1])$ is not Choquet. These results answers the previously posed questions.

    We study the partition regular properties of topological groups, proving several extensions of Hindman theorem where monochromatic sets of finite sums are required to satisfy additional topological constraints. In particular, our results imply that for every nowhere dense set $C \subseteq \mathbb{R}^n$, there exists an open set $P \supseteq C$ such that for any finite coloring of $\mathbb{Q}^n \setminus P$, there is a family $\mathcal{A}$ of sequences in $\mathbb{Q}^n \setminus P$ which satisfies the following properties: (i) for each $A\in\mathcal A$, the set $\operatorname{FS}(A)$ of finite sums of $A$ is a closed discrete subset of $\mathbb R^n$; (ii) the set $\bigcup_{A\in\mathcal A}\operatorname{FS}(A)$ is monochromatic; and (iii) the set $\bigcup_{A\in\mathcal A}\operatorname{FS}(A)$ is dense in an open unbounded subset of $\mathbb R^n$.

  3. [2512.11810] 无穷远处的加权上确界范数：穷竭函数、衰减类和异向端
    Weighted Sup-Norms at Infinity: Exhaustion Functions, Decay Classes, and Anisotropic Ends
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究加权上确界范数和衰减类。
    • 证明约化定理，乘法是等距。
    • 纠正对偶和穷竭函数的期望。
    • 刻画粗略仿射不变性。
    • 对多端空间提出端对端泛函。
    💡 研究非紧空间上加权上确界范数，证明约化定理并纠正关于对偶和穷竭函数的两个自然期望。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于非紧、局部紧、σ-紧的Hausdorff空间，具有连续适当的穷竭函数和容许权重，我们研究加权上确界范数，衡量函数在无穷远处接近其极限的速度，以及它们模常数的商。我们的主要结果是约化定理：当权重无界时，常数上的下确界在无穷远极限处达到，乘以权重是到有界连续函数的满射线性等距，将衰减类映射到在无穷远处消失的函数。因此，完备性、对偶性、紧性和截断的稠密性是通过这个等距看到的经典事实。然后我们纠正两个自然期望。加权零质量测度等距嵌入衰减类的对偶中具有稠密值域，但不是整个对偶；范数测度恰好当变换后的函数达到其最大值和负值时存在，这通常失败。并且适当的穷竭函数一般不粗略仿射等价，因此衰减分类取决于穷竭函数；我们通过权重沿穷竭函数像的膨胀条件来刻画粗略仿射不变性。对于具有有限多个端的空间，只要两个端携带不同极限，单常数商就是无限的；我们用一个端对端泛函替换它，该泛函具有结构定理和完备性。附录列出了相对于发布版本所做的更正。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a non-compact, locally compact, sigma-compact Hausdorff space with a continuous proper exhaustion function, and an admissible weight, we study the weighted supremum norms measuring how fast a function approaches its limit at infinity, and their quotient modulo constants. Our main result is a reduction theorem: when the weight is unbounded, the infimum over constants is attained at the limit at infinity, and multiplication by the weight is a surjective linear isometry onto the bounded continuous functions, carrying the decay class onto the functions vanishing at infinity. Completeness, duality, compactness and density of truncations are therefore classical facts read through this isometry. We then correct two natural expectations. The weighted zero-mass measures embed isometrically into the dual of the decay class with dense range, but are not the whole dual; norming measures exist exactly when the transformed function attains both its supremum and its negative, which fails generically. And proper exhaustions are not in general coarsely affinely equivalent, so the decay classification depends on the exhaustion; we characterise coarse-affine invariance by a dilation condition on the weight along the image of the exhaustion. For spaces with finitely many ends, the single-constant quotient is infinite as soon as two ends carry different limits; we replace it by an end-by-end functional admitting a structure theorem and completeness. An appendix lists the corrections made with respect to the posted version.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11810

    📖 中文摘要

    设J_r^{SU(n)}(K;q)表示零框架纽结K的约化SU(n)量子不变量，用定义表示的r次对称幂着色，并归一化为未结的1。对于每个固定的n≥2，我们证明Chen-Liu-Zhu分圆展开猜想：存在唯一系数H_k^{(n)}(K;q)∈Z[q^{±1}]使得J_r^{SU(n)}(K;q)=∑_{k=0}^{r}(∏_{i=0}^{k-1}{r-i}{r+n+i})H_k^{(n)}(K;q)，其中{m}=q^m-q^{-m}。Beliakova-Gorsky的有限对偶插值公式给出积分单侧阶乘展开。在将它们的约化标量与Habiro-Lê约定识别后，我们将完备中心限制到单行颜色。完备Harish-Chandra反射然后在变量z中产生反转对称性，通过X=z+z^{-1}的积分下降将单侧展开转换为双侧牛顿基。分圆局部插值和UFD分母移除论证证明牛顿系数的Laurent整性。我们还确定了秩一致展开的自然系数环。对于每个零框架纽结，存在唯一Laurent微分系数G_k(K;A,q)∈Z[A^{±1},q^{±1}]。相关的牛顿系数是A上的Laurent多项式，系数在Q(q)中，其在每个几何节点A=q^n（n≥2）处的值位于Z[q^{±1}]中。它们定义了一个双变量牛顿逆极限元素，其正秩特化恢复所有对称色HOMFLY-PT多项式。完备化在牛顿核中进行，而不是在单位根处逐系数进行。在固定正秩和颜色后，级数是有限的，可以在单位根处求值。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $J_r^{SU(n)}(K;q)$ denote the reduced $SU(n)$ quantum invariant of a zero-framed knot $K$, colored by the $r$th symmetric power of the defining representation and normalized to be $1$ for the unknot. For every fixed $n\ge2$ we prove the Chen--Liu--Zhu cyclotomic expansion conjecture: there are unique coefficients $H_k^{(n)}(K;q)\in\mathbb{Z}[q^{\pm1}]$ such that \[ J_r^{SU(n)}(K;q)=\sum_{k=0}^{r} \left(\prod_{i=0}^{k-1}\{r-i\}\{r+n+i\}\right) H_k^{(n)}(K;q), \] where $\{m\}=q^m-q^{-m}$. The finite dual interpolation formula of Beliakova--Gorsky gives an integral one-sided factorial expansion. After identifying their reduced scalar with the Habiro--L\^e convention, we restrict the completed center to one-row colors. Completed Harish--Chandra reflection then yields inversion symmetry in the variable $z$, and integral descent through $X=z+z^{-1}$ converts the one-sided expansion into the two-sided Newton basis. Cyclotomic-local interpolation and a UFD denominator-removal argument prove Laurent integrality of the Newton coefficients. We also determine a natural coefficient ring for a rank-uniform expansion. For every zero-framed knot there are unique Laurent differential coefficients $G_k(K;A,q)\in\mathbb{Z}[A^{\pm1},q^{\pm1}]$. The associated Newton coefficients are Laurent polynomials in $A$ over $\mathbb{Q}(q)$ whose values at every geometric node $A=q^n$, $n\ge2$, lie in $\mathbb{Z}[q^{\pm1}]$. They define a two-variable Newton inverse-limit element whose positive-rank specializations recover all symmetric-color HOMFLY--PT polynomials. The completion is taken in the Newton kernels rather than coefficientwise at roots of unity. After a positive rank and a color have been fixed, the series is finite and may be evaluated at a root of unity.

    给定任何闭双曲n流形M（n≥3），我们证明对于固定的κ>3/(n-2)，当r足够大时，长度接近r的随机闭测地线具有宽度r^{-κ}的领口。特别是，大多数长度接近r的闭测地线是简单的。这个定理肯定地回答了Kirby列表中的问题3.16，该问题询问每个闭双曲3流形是否包含无限多条简单闭测地线。

    Given any closed hyperbolic n-manifold M ($n\ge 3$), we show that for a fixed $\kappa>\frac{3}{n-2}$, a random closed geodesic of length close to r has the collar of width $r^{-\kappa}$ when r is large enough. In particular, most closed geodesics of length close to r are simple. This theorem positively answers Problem 3.16 from Kirby's list which asks whether every closed hyperbolic 3-manifold contains infinitely many simple closed geodesics.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29761

We review the affine analogue of the flat conformal structures on the torus through the work of Oliver Baues and William Goldman, and define an open subset of this deformation space corresponding to markings of complete affine tilings of the plane. We make this definition precise, and provide algebraic conditions on the symmetries of the tiling to define it. We then analyze the dynamics of symplectic tiling billiards played on these types of tilings and investigate the long-term dynamics of the system to prove a stability result concerning divergent trajectories. The divergence is defined in terms of geometric invariants arising from the tiling symmetry group. We argue that in some sense this divergence is a consequence of the tiles of a non-Euclidean tiling becoming 'thin' as one moves far away in the tiling. To do so we introduce a notion of thinness that is well adapted to the non-Euclidean affine tilings.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们提出了从三维中接触结构对构造余维一叶状结构的新方法。这构成了Eliashberg和Thurston关于叶状结构被接触结构逼近的著名定理的逆结果。在初始接触对的适当假设下，我们构造的叶状结构是紧的，使我们能够完全用接触几何来刻画紧叶状结构的存在性。这一观点揭示了紧叶状结构的一些令人惊讶的灵活性现象，并为L-空间猜想提供了新的见解。证明的第一部分建立在Colin和Firmo关于正接触对的工作之上。第二部分涉及Burago和Ivanov关于构造与连续平面场相切的分支叶状结构的技术结果的广泛推广，可能具有独立的意义。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We present a new construction of codimension-one foliations from pairs of contact structures in dimension three. This constitutes a converse result to a celebrated theorem of Eliashberg and Thurston on approximations of foliations by contact structures. Under suitable hypotheses on the initial contact pairs, the foliations we construct are taut, allowing us to characterize the existence of taut foliations entirely in terms of contact geometry. This viewpoint reveals some surprising flexibility phenomena for taut foliations, and provides new insight into the $L$-space conjecture. The first part of the proof builds upon the work of Colin and Firmo on positive contact pairs.     📖 中文摘要

    我们研究李群的渐近锥，展示了权重的几何与渐近锥的高度非单连通性之间的联系。我们证明，一个李群若有一个SOL型群作为商，则其渐近锥的基本群包含夏威夷耳环空间的基本群。我们还得到了渐近锥的第一同调群的强非零结果。对李群的假设由Abels引入，称为“SOL障碍”，等价于权重上的一个显式几何条件。我们的工作建立在Burillo的结果之上，他证明了SOL情形下渐近锥的陈述，以及Cornulier和Tessera的结果，他们表明SOL障碍意味着Dehn函数的指数增长。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study asymptotic cones of Lie groups, presenting a link between the geometry of the weights and the highly non-simply-connected nature of asymptotic cones. We show that a Lie group that admits a group of SOL-type as a quotient is such that the fundamental group of its asymptotic cone contains the fundamental group of the Hawaiian earring space. We also obtain a strong non-vanishing result for the first homology group of the asymptotic cone. The assumption on the Lie group, introduced by Abels and called the "SOL obstruction", is equivalent to an explicit geometric condition on the weights. Our work builds upon the results of Burillo, who proves the statement on asymptotic cones in the case of SOL, and on those of Cornulier and Tessera who show that the SOL obstruction implies exponential growth of the Dehn function.

  2. [2308.13635] 字母编织：连接组合群论与拓扑
    Letter-braiding: bridging combinatorial group theory and topology
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文定义了字母编织不变量，度量词中字母的交错。
    • 该不变量完全检测维数序列，且是万有有限型不变量。
    • 来源于杠构造，适用于多种背景。
    • 应用于定义Johnson过滤和同态的变体，约束有限p-群自同构。
    💡 定义任意群中词的字母编织不变量，完全检测维数序列，并应用于自同构群。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们定义了任意群中词的不变量，度量词中字母的交错方式，完全检测群的维数序列。这些是字母编织不变量。在自由群上，编织不变量与Magnus展开中的系数一致。与Magnus系数不同，我们的不变量定义在所有群上，且在任何PID上。它们尊重群中的乘积，并且是维数序列的完全不变量，因此它们是万有多项式有限型不变量的系数，函子性地依赖于群。字母编织不变量来源于具有给定基本群的空间的余链模型的杠构造。这种方法适用于群的单纯表示以及更几何的背景，我们在例子中说明。作为一个应用，我们定义了任意群自同构上的Johnson过滤和Johnson同态的变体，并用它们来约束有限p-群的自同构。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We define invariants of words in arbitrary groups, measuring how letters in a word are interleaving, perfectly detecting the dimension series of a group. These are the letter-braiding invariants. On free groups, braiding invariants coincide with coefficients in the Magnus expansion. In contrast with Magnus' coefficients, our invariants are defined on all groups and over any PID. They respect products in the group and are a complete invariant of the dimension series, so they are the coefficients of a universal multiplicative finite-type invariant, depending functorially on the group. Letter-braiding invariants arise from the bar construction on a cochain model of a space with a prescribed fundamental group. This approach specializes to simplicial presentations of a group as well as to more geometric contexts, which we illustrate in examples. As an application, we define variants of the Johnson filtration and the Johnson homomorphism on the automorphisms of arbitrary groups, and use them to constrain automorphisms of finite p-groups.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.13635

    Decidability of the Orbit Problem Over $\mathbb{Q}(X)$
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文证明了Q(X)上的轨道问题可判定。
    • 推广了Kannan-Lipton的结果。
    • 定义了群的幂轨道问题。
    • 证明了辫群和Aut(F_2)的幂轨道问题可判定。
    💡 证明有理函数域上的轨道问题可判定，并应用于群中的幂轨道问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    轨道问题是给定x, y∈Q^n和n×n矩阵A，是否存在i∈N使得A^i x = y的问题。1980年，Kannan和Lipton证明了轨道问题是可判定的。我们证明轨道问题的一个推广，其中域是Q(X)（X为可数个超越元），也是可判定的。我们将任意群G的幂轨道问题定义为：给定x, y∈G，是否存在n∈Z使得x^n = y。然后我们利用主要结果证明幂轨道问题对于一系列群是可判定的，包括辫群和Aut(F_2)。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The orbit problem is the problem of whether, given $x, y\in \mathbb{Q}^n$ and an $n\times n$ matrix $A$, there exists an $i\in\mathbb{N}$ such that $A^ix = y$. In 1980, Kannan and Lipton proved that the orbit problem is decidable. We show that a generalization of the orbit problem, where the field is $\mathbb{Q}(X)$ for $X$ a countable set of transcendentals, is also decidable. We define the orbit power problem for an arbitrary group $G$ to be the problem of when, given $x, y\in G$, there exists an $n\in\mathbb{Z}$ such that $x^n = y$. We then use the main result to show that the power orbit problem is decidable for an assortment of groups, including the braid groups and $\operatorname{Aut}(F_2)$.

    💡 构造具有二次共轭元长度函数和特定Dehn函数的有限表示群，展示两个不变量独立性。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明对于任何具有超加性时间函数f的识别S-机S，存在一个有限表示群，其共轭元长度函数是二次的，且其Dehn函数增长如f的平方。这个结果与作者之前一篇论文的结果对偶，该论文构造了一族具有三次Dehn函数和不同共轭元长度函数的群。我们因此给出了第一个已知的有限表示群的例子，其共轭元长度函数是递归的，但字问题和共轭问题都不可判定。这回答了Rips问题的一个类比。此外，给定一个具有可判定字问题的有限表示群G，我们得到一个具有可判定共轭问题的有限表示群，其Dehn函数增长快于G的Dehn函数。最后，将此结果与其对偶结合，对于广泛的函数对(f,g)，我们提供了一个有限表示群的例子，其Dehn函数等价于f，共轭元长度函数等价于g。这表明这两个不变量非常强地独立，并在Bridson、Riley和Sale的问题上取得了重大进展。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We show that for any recognizing $S$-machine $\textbf{S}$ with superadditive time function $f$, there exists a finitely presented group whose conjugator length function is quadratic and whose Dehn function grows like the square of $f$. This result is dual to that of a previous paper of the authors, which constructed a family of groups with cubic Dehn function that have various conjugator length functions. We thereby give the first known example of a finitely presented group whose conjugator length function is recursive, but whose Word and Conjugacy Problems are both undecidable. This answers an analogue of a question of Rips. Moreover, given a finitely presented group $G$ with decidable Word Problem, we obtain a finitely presented group with decidable Conjugacy Problem whose Dehn function grows faster than that of $G$. Finally, combining this result with its dual, for a wide array of pairs of functions $(f,g)$ we furnish an example of a finitely presented group with Dehn function equivalent to $f$ and conjugator length function equivalent to $g$. This shows that the two invariants are very strongly independent and making significant progress on a question of Bridson, Riley, and Sale.

The second combines Nakamura's criterion with, respectively, flag-space and Roller-boundary dynamics, yielding infinite-girth results for semisimple $S$-algebraic groups and for essential non-elementary actions on finite-dimensional, second countable, non-Euclidean CAT(0) cube complexes. We also prove that every finitely generated large group has infinite girth, thereby answering a question of Akhmedov and Mishra.

    Finite-Monoid Compression in Syntactic Concept Lattices: Arity Hierarchies and a Pseudovariety Trichotomy
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究句法概念格中有限幺半群压缩的元数层次。
    • 将压缩数刻画为关系态射的陪域基数。
    • 证明压缩高度三分法：1、2或无穷。
    • 无穷情形与Synt({ab})相关，有限情形有具体稳定结果。
    • 一元情形与图色数联系，判定问题NP完全。
    💡 研究了有限幺半群压缩在句法概念格中的行为，得到压缩高度三分法。
    📖 中文摘要

    Clark的句法概念格(SCL)记录双侧分布结构，Wurm将其扩展到任意有限元数的元组。我们研究cmp_f(L)，即在主层上通过元数f保持有界元组替换的有限幺半群观测的最小像大小。对于正则语言，我们将cmp_f(L)精确刻画为从尖点句法幺半群到某个f-分离关系态射的陪域的最小基数。令ch(V)表示压缩数在伪簇V上一致稳定所需的最小元数。我们的主要结果是可能的一致高度的三分法：ch(V)∈{1,2,∞}，且ch(V)=∞当且仅当Synt({ab})∈V。因此不存在有限的统一压缩高度3,4,...。无限情形是尖锐的：在⟨Synt({ab})⟩内部，每个边界d→d+1都有无界压缩间隙，且元数层次的任意有限严格前缀都可实现。在有限侧，交换幺半群和带在元数1处稳定，而每个完全正则句法幺半群在元数2处稳定；有限群核表明二元界是尖锐的。在一元元数，每个非空有限简单图都由一个显式的长度三语言实现，得到精确的色数公式和判定cmp_1(L)≤3的NP完全性。压缩高度1和2之间的结构边界仍然开放。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Clark's syntactic concept lattice (SCL) records two-sided distributional structure, and Wurm extended it to tuples of arbitrary finite arity. We study \(\operatorname{cmp}_f(L)\), the minimum image size of a finite-monoid observation that preserves guarded tuple substitution through arity \(f\) on the principal layer. For regular languages, we characterize \(\operatorname{cmp}_f(L)\) exactly as the least cardinality of the codomain of an \(f\)-separating relational morphism from the pointed syntactic monoid. Let \(\operatorname{ch}(\mathbf V)\) denote the least arity at which these compression numbers stabilize uniformly over a pseudovariety \(\mathbf V\). Our main result is the following trichotomy of possible uniform heights: \(\operatorname{ch}(\mathbf V)\in\{1,2,\infty\}\), with \(\operatorname{ch}(\mathbf V)=\infty\) if and only if \(\operatorname{Synt}(\{ab\})\in\mathbf V\). Thus no finite uniform compression height \(3,4,\ldots\) occurs. The infinite case is sharp: inside \(\langle\operatorname{Synt}(\{ab\})\rangle\), every boundary \(d\to d+1\) admits unbounded compression gaps, and arbitrary finite strict prefixes of the arity hierarchy are realizable. On the finite side, commutative monoids and bands stabilize at arity one, while every completely regular syntactic monoid stabilizes by arity two; finite group kernels show that the binary bound is sharp. At unary arity, every nonempty finite simple graph is realized by an explicit length-three language, yielding an exact chromatic-number formula and NP-completeness of deciding \(\operatorname{cmp}_1(L)\le 3\) for explicitly listed length-three languages. The structural boundary between compression heights one and two remains open.

    • 在具体模型中给出显式结果。
    💡 系统研究对偶Burnside链，证明其性质并应用于压缩。
    📖 中文摘要

    Burnside过程是用于从群轨道均匀采样的经典马尔可夫链。我们系统研究对偶Burnside过程，通过交换群元素和状态的角色获得。该对偶链具有平稳律π(g)∝|X_g|，是可逆的，并允许矩阵分解Q=AB，K=BA，其中K是经典Burnside核。因此，两条链共享所有非零特征值，且混合时间至多相差一步。我们进一步建立普遍的Doeblin下界、轨道和共轭类 lumping、精确的稳定子/不动集商对，以及Q和K之间的转移原理。我们分析了值置换模型S_k作用于[k]^n和坐标置换模型S_n作用于[k]^n的显式例子。在值置换模型中，对于固定k≥3，对偶固定符号集商有2^k-k-1个状态，与n无关，保持完整的非零谱，且极限非平凡谱半径为1/2。这些结果表明对偶链既提供了经典Burnside过程的概念镜像，也提供了对称感知MCMC的真正有用的压缩机制。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The Burnside process is a classical Markov chain for sampling uniformly from group orbits. We give a systematic study of the dual Burnside process, obtained by interchanging the roles of group elements and states. This dual chain has stationary law $\pi(g)\propto |X_g|$, is reversible, and admits a matrix factorization $Q=AB$, $K=BA$ with the classical Burnside kernel $K$. As a consequence, the two chains share all nonzero eigenvalues and have mixing times that differ by at most one step. We further establish universal Doeblin floors, orbit- and conjugacy-class lumpings, exact stabilizer/fixed-set quotient pairs, and transfer principles between $Q$ and $K$. We analyze the explicit examples of the value-permutation model $S_k$ acting on $[k]^n$ and the coordinate-permutation model $S_n$ acting on $[k]^n$. In the value-permutation model, for fixed $k\ge3$, the dual fixed-symbol-set quotient has $2^k-k-1$ states, independent of $n$, preserves the full nonzero spectrum, and has limiting nontrivial spectral radius $1/2$. These results show that the dual chain provides both a conceptual mirror to the classical Burnside process and a genuinely useful compression mechanism for symmetry-aware Markov chain Monte Carlo.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25202

    我们研究球凸体的经典Sylvester四点问题，其中凸包被固定半径R的球交代替。我们证明Efron-Buchta恒等式在此框架中持续成立，因此n个均匀随机点的R-球凸包的顶点数分布由其子样本的凸包体积矩决定，反之亦然。对于任意平面球凸体中的三个和四个均匀随机点，我们确定了R-球凸包的Sylvester概率。我们还给出了Groemer不等式的扩展，用于n个随机点的球凸包的期望面积，并证明它由同面积的圆盘最小化。特别地，这产生了球凸模拟的边界曲线的仿射长度的等周不等式。对于单位圆盘，我们可以用半径R的双对数函数表达Sylvester概率，并在R=1时给出显式值。当R→∞时，这些恢复经典Sylvester概率。作为另一个结果，我们确定了圆盘中三个均匀随机点的外接半径在[1,∞)上的精确分布函数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We investigate Sylvester's classical four-point problem for ball-convex bodies, where convex hulls are replaced by intersections of balls of a fixed radius $R$. We show that the Efron--Buchta identities persist in this framework, so that the distribution of the number of vertices of the $R$-ball-convex hull of $n$ uniform random points is determined by the moments of the volumes of the hulls of its subsamples, and vice versa. For three and four uniform random points in an arbitrary planar ball-convex body, we determine the Sylvester probabilities of the $R$-ball-convex hull. We also give an extension of Groemer's inequality for the expected area of the ball-convex hull of $n$ random points and show that it is minimised by the disc of the same area. In particular, this yields an isoperimetric inequality for the ball-convex analogue of the affine length of the boundary curve. For the unit disc, we can express the Sylvester probabilities using dilogarithmic functions of the radius $R$, and give explicit values at $R=1$. As $R\to\infty$, these recover the classical Sylvester probabilities. As another consequence, we determine the exact distribution function of the circumradius of three uniform random points in he disc on $[1,\infty)$.

    🤖 AI 总结

    💡 证明Heisenberg群中内蕴Lipschitz图的几何引理，推广到任意维数。
    📖 中文摘要

    设1≤k≤n。我们证明Heisenberg群H^n中的k维内蕴Lipschitz图满足水平β数的几何引理GLem(β_{2,V_k},p)，指数p=p(k)。此前，此结果仅在k=1时已知；我们的证明在此情形恢复了尖锐指数p=4。对于k>1，我们改编了Orponen最初为欧氏和抛物Lipschitz函数发展的积分几何方法。此外，对于k=n，我们展示了如何利用Morrey型不等式直接从R^{2n}中的各向同性Dorronsoro定理推导几何引理。基于新的几何引理，我们建立了H^n中k-正则集允许由内蕴Lipschitz图进行corona分解的必要条件。该条件已知由前两位作者与Pinamonti的早期工作充分。它涉及除β_{2,V_k}之外的额外平坦系数。在此过程中，我们将几何引理在“大块”函子下的已知稳定性结果扩展到更大的系数类。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $1\leq k\leq n$. We prove that $k$-dimensional intrinsic Lipschitz graphs in the Heisenberg group $\mathbb{H}^n$ satisfy a geometric lemma $\mathrm{GLem}(\beta_{2,\mathcal{V}_k},p)$ for horizontal $\beta$-numbers with an exponent $p=p(k)$. Previously, this result was known only in the case $k=1$; our proof recovers the sharp exponent $p=4$ in this setting. For $k>1$, we adapt an integral geometric approach originally developed by Orponen for Euclidean and parabolic Lipschitz functions. In addition, for $k=n$, we show how to deduce a geometric lemma directly from an isotropic Dorronsoro theorem in $\mathbb{R}^{2n}$ using a Morrey-type inequality. Building on the new geometric lemmas, we establish a necessary condition for $k$-regular sets in $\mathbb{H}^n$ to admit corona decompositions by intrinsic Lipschitz graphs. The condition is known to be sufficient by earlier work of the last two authors together with Pinamonti. It involves additional flatness coefficients besides $\beta_{2,\mathcal{V}_k}$. Along the way, we therefore extend the known stability results for geometric lemmas under the "big pieces'' functor to a larger class of coefficients.

  5. [2608.30365] 具有非负权重的有限度量空间的magnitude连续性
    Continuity of the magnitude for finite metric spaces with nonnegative weightings
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究有限度量空间magnitude的连续性。
    • 证明非负权重类中对数magnitude Lipschitz连续。
    • magnitude本身局部Lipschitz但非全局。
    💡 证明具有非负权重的有限度量空间的magnitude对数Lipschitz连续。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于有限度量空间，magnitude是一个已知在Gromov-Hausdorff距离下处处不连续的 invariant。虽然大部分文献关注正定度量空间，这里我们考虑允许非负权重的有限度量空间。本文关注允许非负权重的有限度量空间类。该类包括超度量空间，已知其magnitude有特定界。我们通过证明对于任何两个具有非负权重的有限度量空间，它们的magnitude之比被其Gromov-Hausdorff距离指数界来推广这些界。因此，我们证明在此类中，对数magnitude函数是Lipschitz连续的，magnitude函数本身是局部Lipschitz连续的，但不是Lipschitz连续的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For finite metric spaces, magnitude is an invariant known to be discontinuous everywhere with respect to the Gromov-Hausdorff distance. While much of the literature focuses on positive definite metric spaces, here we consider finite metric spaces admitting a nonnegative weighting. In this paper, we focus on the class of finite metric spaces that admit a nonnegative weighting. This class includes ultrametric spaces, for which specific bounds on magnitude are known. We generalize these bounds by proving that, for any two finite metric spaces with nonnegative weightings, the ratio of their magnitudes is exponentially bounded by their Gromov-Hausdorff distance. Consequently, we show that within this class, the logarithmic magnitude function is Lipschitz continuous, and the magnitude function itself is locally Lipschitz continuous, but not Lipschitz continuous.

    Approximate Gromov--Hausdorff continuity of magnitude and weighting
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究magnitude在Gromov-Hausdorff扰动下的行为。
    • 证明在测度论意义下近似连续。
    • 权重近似收敛。
    💡 证明magnitude在测度论意义下近似连续，权重近似收敛。
    📖 中文摘要

    Magnitude给出了有限度量空间的有效大小，现在用于多个数据分析场景。对于此类应用，自然要问magnitude在Gromov-Hausdorff扰动下如何表现，包括点的碰撞。然而，magnitude在有限度量空间上处处不连续。我们证明这种失败在精确的测度论意义上是非典型的。在固定每个碰撞簇中的点数后，magnitude近似连续。更强地，当点簇碰撞到极限空间的点时，每个簇的总权重近似收敛到相应极限点的权重。主要工具是关于奇异矩阵逆的近似极限的一般结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Magnitude gives an effective size of a finite metric space and is now used in several data-analysis settings. For such applications, it is natural to ask how magnitude behaves under Gromov-Hausdorff perturbations, including collisions of points. However, magnitude is nowhere continuous on finite metric spaces with the Gromov--Hausdorff topology. We show that this failure is nongeneric in a precise measure-theoretic sense. After fixing the number of points in each collapsing cluster, magnitude is approximately continuous. More strongly, when clusters of points collapse to the points of a limit space, the total weighting of each cluster approximately converges to the weighting of the corresponding limit point. The main tool is a general result on approximate limits of inverses near singular matrices.

    Repairing the refined-decoupling proof of the 5/4 planar pinned Falconer theorem
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 指出精化解耦估计的任意包形式错误。
    • 给出显式反例。
    • 通过扩张稳定任意包定理修复证明。
    • 保持指数-(α+1)/3，闭合能量论证。
    💡 指出原证明中的错误并给出修复，保持指数-（α+1）/3。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们表明Guth-Iosevich-Ou-Wang证明平面钉扎Falconer定理中打印的精化解耦估计的字面任意包形式是错误的，即使其管维度被归一化。一个显式的 collar 构造给出了固定幂反例：所有包在一个正方形上活跃，而它们的较小标记管都不与该正方形相交。然后我们给出原始证明路径的非圆形修复。分析输入是一个扩张稳定的任意包定理，其中包集中在a-膨胀上，重数用严格更大的b-膨胀计数。我们通过加权ℓ²解耦的归纳直接证明该定理，跟踪通过抛物重标度的膨胀余量。修正后的定理直接适用于原始Falconer父包，经过精确频率截断和绝对小包截止；不需要父到规范分解。然后我们重建好管关联估计，保留局部常值强制的邻域，并提供一致正则化和极限论证。主频率指数保持-(α+1)/3，因此能量论证在α>5/4时精确闭合。钉扎定理本身未被矛盾，也通过后来的微局域方法已知。后来的规范波包处理精化解耦与活动管观点重叠，但不与反例或修复的Falconer证明链重叠。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We show that the literal arbitrary-packet form of the refined-decoupling estimate printed in the Guth--Iosevich-Ou-Wang proof of the planar pinned Falconer theorem is false, even after its tube dimensions are normalized. An explicit collar construction gives a fixed-power counterexample: all packets are active on one square while none of their smaller labelled tubes meets that square. We then give a non circular repair of the original proof route. The analytic input is an enlargement-stable arbitrary-packet theorem in which a packet is concentrated on an $a$-dilate and multiplicity is counted with a strictly larger $b$-dilate. We prove this theorem directly from weighted $\ell^2$ decoupling by an induction that tracks the dilation margin through parabolic rescaling. The corrected theorem applies directly to the original Falconer parent packets after an exact frequency truncation and an absolute small-packet cutoff; no parent-to-canonical decomposition is needed. We then rebuild the good-tube incidence estimate, retain the neighborhood forced by local constancy, and supply a uniform regularization and limiting argument. The principal frequency exponent remains $-(\alpha+1)/3$, so the energy argument closes exactly for $\alpha>5/4$. The pinned theorem itself is not contradicted and is also known through later microlocal methods. A later canonical wave-packet treatment of refined decoupling overlaps with the activity-tube viewpoint but not with the counterexample or the repaired Falconer proof chain.

  8. [2608.28770] 无逐点Fourier衰减的凸曲线Minkowski和
    Minkowski sums with convex curves without pointwise Fourier decay
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究凸曲线Minkowski和的阈值。
    • 构造无Fourier衰减但阈值最优的例子。
    • 利用正曲率迹机制证明正测度结果。
    • 提出主要开放问题。
    💡 构造严格凸曲线，其Minkowski和阈值最优但无Fourier衰减。
    📖 中文摘要

    设Γ⊂R²是紧凸图，定义T(Γ)=inf{t: dim_H(E)>t ⇒ |E+Γ|>0 对所有紧E⊂R²}。对于正长度区间上的图，最小可能值是T(Γ)=1。我们问当弧长逐点Fourier衰减不可用时，这个最优结论能否成立。答案是肯定的，即使对于严格凸曲线。我们使用Fourier变换约定ν̂(ξ)=∫e^{-2πix·ξ}dν(x)。我们构造一个严格凸Lipschitz图Γ，T(Γ)=1，使得对于每个非平凡子弧Γ₀和每个α>0，limsup_{|ξ|→∞}|ξ|^α|H¹|_{Γ₀}(ξ)|=∞。我们还给出一个凸例子，其中每个非平凡子弧上的弧长甚至不是Rajchman测度。这些例子背后的几何机制是正曲率迹：如果Γ包含一个C²曲线的正长度子集，其曲率有界远离零，则当dim_H(E)>1时|E+Γ|>0。对于非退化图，这给出T(Γ)=1。对于凸图，它特别意味着当曲率测度有非零绝对连续部分时T(Γ)=1。正测度证明在物理空间中使用平移管交和凸函数的基本事实。相同的重叠估计在正曲率迹假设下给出邻域曲线投影平均长度的Mattila型下界。我们还证明了一个具有正长度可求长部分的集合的一维端点结果，并表述了主要剩余问题：是否每个严格凸Lipschitz图都有最优阈值T(Γ)=1。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\Gamma\subset\mathbb R^2$ be a compact convex graph and define \[ T(\Gamma) = \inf \left\{ t: \dim_{\mathrm H}(E)>t \Longrightarrow |E+\Gamma|>0 \text{ for every compact }E\subset\mathbb R^2 \right\}. \] For a graph over an interval of positive length the smallest possible value is $T(\Gamma)=1$. We ask whether this optimal conclusion can hold when pointwise Fourier decay of arclength is unavailable. The answer is yes, even for strictly convex curves. We use the Fourier transform convention $\widehat\nu(\xi)=\int e^{-2\pi i x\cdot\xi}\,d\nu(x)$. We construct a strictly convex Lipschitz graph $\Gamma$ with $T(\Gamma)=1$ such that, for every nontrivial subarc $\Gamma_0$ and every $\alpha>0$, \[ \limsup_{|\xi|\to\infty} |\xi|^\alpha \left| \widehat{H^1|_{\Gamma_0}}(\xi) \right|= \infty. \] We also give a convex example for which arclength on every nontrivial subarc fails even to be a Rajchman measure. The geometric mechanism behind these examples is a positive curved trace: if $\Gamma$ contains a positive-length subset of a $C^2$ curve whose curvature is bounded away from zero, then $|E+\Gamma|>0$ whenever $\dim_{\mathrm H}(E)>1$. For a nondegenerate graph this gives $T(\Gamma)=1$. For convex graphs it implies, in particular, that $T(\Gamma)=1$ whenever the curvature measure has a nonzero absolutely continuous part. The positive-measure proofs are in physical space and use translated-tube intersections and elementary facts about convex functions. The same overlap estimates give Mattila-type lower bounds for the average lengths of the associated curve projections of neighborhoods under the positive curved-trace hypothesis. We also prove a dimension-one endpoint result for sets with a positive-length rectifiable part and formulate the main remaining question: whether every strictly convex Lipschitz graph has the optimal threshold $T(\Gamma)=1$.

    🤖 AI 总结
