    📖 中文摘要

    海岸线搜索路径从原点开始，必须遇到一条未知仿射线，不知道其法线或距离。我们首先为齐次搜索问题建立自相似约化定理，其历史信息是由逐点最大值更新的记录剖面。归一化状态的两个准返回界定一个块，该块通过自身更新所需剖面；一个短连接器将此块闭合为胞腔。因此，每个有限比路径都可以通过单个胞腔在所有尺度上的重复以任意小损失近似。然后主链变得有效。状态空间的有限编码可计算地界定近最优胞腔的尺度因子和归一化长度。对于平面海岸线搜索，凸包的支持函数给出精确的胞腔泛函。单侧多边形化将问题简化为可计算数量的顶点，之后量词消去决定是否存在低于有理阈值的多边形胞腔。因此，最优确定性平面海岸线值C₂*是可计算实数：对于每个有理ε>0，算法终止于包含C₂*的宽度至多ε的有理区间。附加结果——滑动记忆、Bellman转移、截止时间、几何滤波器和相对平衡——作为认证计算和螺旋刚性研究的工具箱单独呈现；它们不用于可计算性证明。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A shoreline-search path starts at the origin and must meet an unknown affine line, without knowing either its normal or its distance. We first establish a self-similar reduction theorem for homogeneous search problems whose historical information is a record profile updated by pointwise maximum. Two quasi-returns of the normalized state delimit a block that renews the required profile by itself; a short connector closes this block into a cell. Every finite-ratio path can therefore be approximated, with arbitrarily small loss, by repetitions of a single cell at all scales. The main chain is then made effective. A finite coding of the state space computably bounds the scale factor and normalized length of a nearly optimal cell. For planar Shoreline search, the support function of the convex hull gives an exact cell functional. A one-sided polygonalization then reduces the problem to a computable number of vertices, after which quantifier elimination decides whether a polygonal cell exists below a rational threshold. It follows that the optimal deterministic planar Shoreline value $C_2^*$ is a computable real: for every rational $\epsilon>0$, an algorithm terminates with a rational interval of width at most $\epsilon$ containing $C_2^*$. Additional results---sliding memory, Bellman transitions, deadlines, geometric filters, and relative equilibria---are presented separately as a toolbox for certified computation and for the study of spiral rigidity; they are not used in the computability proof.

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study online search for an unknown affine hyperplane in $\mathbb{R}^D$, for arbitrary fixed finite dimension. Building on a companion self-similar cell reduction and support-function formulation, we ask how the mechanism changes as the normal space grows from $\mathbb{S}^0$ to $\mathbb{S}^{D-1}$. In $D=1$, alternation and productivity yield an equal-ripple principle and the exact stationary constant $9$. In $D=2$, the analogous relative equilibrium is a logarithmic spiral whose bottleneck chord imposes tangency and selects the pitch. For exponential orbits $\Gamma(\sigma)=e^{\kappa\sigma}\omega(\sigma)$, we develop log-directional geometry, exponentially discounted memory, gauges, and recursive hyperspherical parametrizations. Without a shape ansatz, the bottleneck admits a certificate supported by at most $D$ historical suppliers, and at globally worst phases the current point lies on the active face. Within regular chambers we derive exact variation, tangency, pitch, age, and, in $D=3$, delay-system identities. Odd-dimensional obstructions, antipodal subclasses, and harmonic towers provide constraints and explicit candidate families but are not claimed globally optimal. Finally, the N-COMP theorem shows that $C_D^*$ is a computable real for every fixed finite $D$ and that algebraic polygonal $\varepsilon$-optimal cells can in principle be synthesized. Numerical screening through $D=10$ is kept separate from the proved results.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29060

  14. [2608.29972] Cramér变换、半空间深度与凸体的阈值现象
    Cram\'er transform, half-space depth and threshold phenomena for convex bodies
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究凸体上均匀测度的Cramér变换与半空间深度。
    • 证明尖锐点态比较，阶$\log n$最优。
    • 获得矩、尾估计和采样复杂度联系。
    • 建立方差界和阈值准则，适用于$\ell_p$-球。
    💡 证明凸体上均匀测度的Cramér变换与半空间深度之间的尖锐点态比较，并应用于随机凸包阈值。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究对数凹概率测度的Cramér变换与Tukey半空间深度之间的关系。对于凸体$K\subseteq\mathbb{R}^n$上的均匀概率测度$\mu_K$，我们证明尖锐点态比较$$\Lambda_K^*(x)\leq -\log q_K(x)\leq \Lambda_K^*(x)+\frac12\log n+C,\qquad x\in\operatorname{int}(K),$$其中$C$是绝对常数。$\log n$的阶是最优的，如欧氏球所示。证明结合了指数倾斜、一维对数凹性和Cramér变换的自协调。作为推论，我们获得$\Lambda_K^*$的尖锐阶矩和尾估计，并将$\exp(\Lambda_K^*(x))$（在维数的多项式因子内）与捕获$x$所需的独立样本数联系起来。我们还建立了对数半空间深度的$O(n^2)$方差界，并利用它推导随机凸包尖锐阈值的通用准则。特别地，该准则适用于所有$p>1$的$\ell_p$-球上的均匀测度。这些结果建立了高维凸几何中大偏差代价、几何深度和采样复杂性之间的定量联系。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the relationship between the Cram\'er transform and Tukey's half-space depth for log-concave probability measures. For the uniform probability measure $\mu_K$ on a convex body $K\subseteq\mathbb{R}^n$, we prove the sharp pointwise comparison $$\Lambda_K^*(x)\leq -\log q_K(x)\leq \Lambda_K^*(x)+\frac12\log n+C,\qquad x\in\operatorname{int}(K),$$ where $C$ is an absolute constant. The order $\log n$ is optimal, as shown by the Euclidean ball. The proof combines exponential tilting, one-dimensional log-concavity, and self-concordance of the Cram\'er transform. As consequences, we obtain sharp-order moment and tail estimates for $\Lambda_K^*$ and identify $\exp(\Lambda_K^*(x))$, up to polynomial factors in the dimension, with the number of independent samples needed for $x$ to be captured by their random convex hull. We also establish an $O(n^2)$ variance bound for the logarithmic half-space depth and use it to derive a general criterion for sharp thresholds of random convex hulls. In particular, this criterion applies to the uniform measures on $\ell_p$-balls for every $p>1$. These results establish a quantitative link between large-deviation cost, geometric depth, and sampling complexity in high-dimensional convex geometry.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29972
  15. [2608.30538] 定宽曲线与Lebesgue覆盖问题
    Curves of constant width and Lebesgue's covering problem
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Lebesgue通用覆盖问题，改进面积下界。
    • 使用定宽曲线作为测试集，比经典多边形更优。
    • 通过解析约化和有限计算证明下界0.8344。
    • 计算证书由独立验证器检查，保证严格性。
    💡 用定宽曲线（圆盘、Reuleaux三角形和五边形）改进通用覆盖面积下界至0.8344。
    📖 中文摘要

    通用覆盖是平面中包含直径为一的每个平面集的全等副本的凸集。Lebesgue在1914年要求最小面积，其值未知。我们证明每个凸通用覆盖的面积至少为0.8344，改进了2005年发表的0.832和2026年预印本中的0.833，两者都来自圆盘加上等边三角形和正五边形。我们的测试集改为定宽曲线：圆盘、Reuleaux三角形和Reuleaux五边形。每个都包含其构建的正多边形，因此族在相同数量的体和相同数量的放置参数下严格更大，我们证明经典配置允许一个排列，其凸包面积低于0.8336，因此从这三个集合通过此论证得到的任何界都达不到我们的。定宽曲线在2014年由Gibbs提出用于此角色并进行了数值探索；这里新增的是证明。它由解析约化后跟一个有限计算组成。约化将凸包面积从下方界定在一个放置盒上，通过将每个Reuleaux多边形侵蚀到该盒允许的每个放置中包含的固定集合。计算是对所得五维空间的穷举细分，记录为486,799,600个节点的证书，并由独立于搜索的验证器检查，其浮点误差的严格界比验证达到的余量小数千倍。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A universal cover is a convex set in the plane that contains a congruent copy of every planar set of diameter one. Lebesgue asked in 1914 for one of least area, and the value is not known. We prove that every convex universal cover has area at least 0.8344, improving on 0.832, published in 2005, and 0.833, in a 2026 preprint, both of which come from a disc together with an equilateral triangle and a regular pentagon. Our test sets are instead curves of constant width: the disc, the Reuleaux triangle and the Reuleaux pentagon. Each contains the regular polygon it is built on, so the family is strictly larger at the same number of bodies and the same number of placement parameters, and we show that the classical configuration admits an arrangement whose hull has area below 0.8336, so no bound drawn from those three sets by this argument reaches ours. Curves of constant width were proposed for this role, and explored numerically, by Gibbs in 2014; what is added here is a proof. It consists of an analytic reduction followed by one finite computation. The reduction bounds the hull area from below over an entire box of placements at once, by eroding each Reuleaux polygon to a fixed set contained in every placement that box allows. The computation is an exhaustive subdivision of the resulting five-dimensional space, recorded as a certificate of 486,799,600 nodes and checked by a verifier independent of the search, with a rigorous bound on its floating point error some thousands of times smaller than the margin the verification attains.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30538
  16. [2608.30549] 最大分散测度及其对逼近论中相变的影响
    Maximally Spread Out Measures and Implications for Phase Transitions in Approximation Theory
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 在一般有界子集上构造最大分散测度。
    • 条件为覆盖数增长和拓扑条件。
    • 证明幂指数Hausdorff维数与Minkowski维数一致。
    • 结果适用于支配混合光滑性函数空间。
    💡 在一般（拟）Banach空间的有界子集上构造最大分散测度，证明幂指数Hausdorff维数与Minkowski维数一致。
    📖 中文摘要

    $\newcommand{\X}{\mathbb{X}}\newcommand{\SC}{\mathcal{C}}$ 我们在（拟）Banach空间$\X$的全有界子集$\SC$上建立“最大分散”Borel概率测度的存在性，在两个温和条件下：（i）$\SC$的覆盖数$N(\SC, \epsilon)$的增长条件，和（ii）技术拓扑条件，特别当$\SC\subset\X$是闭、有界、凸时满足。更正式地，条件（i）要求$\SC$的所谓下幂指数Minkowski维数，即$$s_\ast:=\liminf_{\epsilon\downarrow 0}\frac{\log\log N(\SC,\epsilon)}{\log(1/\epsilon)}$$满足$s_\ast>0$。在这些条件下，我们在$\X$上构造一个对$\SC$临界或最大分散的Borel概率测度$\mu$，意味着相关外测度$\mu^\ast$满足$\mu^\ast(\X\setminus\SC)=0$，并且对每个$0<s<s_\ast$满足小球条件$$\mu^\ast(B(x,r))\le\exp\bigl(-c(s)\cdot(1/r)^s\bigr)\quad\text{ 对所有 }x\in\X\text{ 和 }0<r<r_0(s).$$这种临界测度的存在特别意味着[ J. Topol. Anal. 4(2):203--235, 2012]中引入的$\SC$的所谓幂指数Hausdorff维数与下幂指数Minkowski维数一致。先前的工作[Found. Comput. Math. 23(1):329--392, 2023]表明，这种临界测度导致关于有损压缩和通过量化神经网络逼近$\SC$元素的相变，前提是$s_\ast$定义中的$\liminf$作为实际极限存在。在那里，临界测度为某些Besov和Sobolev空间的单位球作为$L^2$的子集构造。相比之下，我们的构造完全一般。特别地，我们的结果适用于支配混合光滑性的函数空间。

    🌐 English Abstract

    $\newcommand{\X}{\mathbb{X}}\newcommand{\SC}{\mathcal{C}}$ We establish the existence of a "maximally spread out" Borel probability measure on a totally bounded subset $\SC$ of a (quasi)-Banach space $\X$ under two mild conditions: (i) a growth condition on the covering numbers $N(\SC, \epsilon)$ of $\SC$, and (ii) a technical topological condition that is in particular satisfied whenever $\SC\subset\X$ is closed, bounded, and convex. More formally, condition (i) requires that the so-called lower power-exponential Minkowski dimension of $\SC$, i.e., \[s_\ast:=\liminf_{\epsilon\downarrow 0}\frac{\log\log N(\SC,\epsilon)}{\log(1/\epsilon)}\] satisfies $s_\ast>0$. Under these conditions, we construct a Borel probability measure $\mu$ on $\X$ that is critical for $\SC$, or maximally spread out, meaning that the associated outer measure $\mu^\ast$ satisfies $\mu^\ast(\X\setminus\SC)= 0$ and furthermore satisfies for every $0<s<s_\ast$ the small-ball condition \[\mu^\ast(B(x,r))\le\exp\bigl(-c(s)\cdot(1/r)^s\bigr)\quad\text{ for all }x\in\X\text{ and }0<r<r_0(s).\] The existence of such a critical measure in particular implies that the so-called power-exponential Hausdorff dimension of $\SC$ introduced in [J.~Topol.~Anal.~4(2):203--235, 2012] coincides with the lower power-exponential Minkowski dimension. Previous work [Found.~Comput.~Math.~23(1):329--392, 2023] shows that such a critical measure gives rise to a phase transition regarding lossy compression and approximation by quantized neural networks of elements of $\SC$, provided that the $\liminf$ in the definition of $s_\ast$ exists as an actual limit. There, critical measures were constructed for unit balls of certain Besov and Sobolev spaces considered as subsets of $L^2$. In contrast, our construction is completely general. In particular, our results apply to function spaces of dominating mixed smoothness.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Spectre铺砌的拓扑和动力学性质。

    📖 中文摘要

    Spectre是欧氏平面的非周期单调块，真正手性，即无需反射块即可铺砌平面。Spectre铺砌的拓扑和动力学性质与Hat铺砌非常相似。具体来说，Spectre位于一个复二维铺砌族中，其中大多数涉及两个形状而非一个。族中所有铺砌给出拓扑共轭动力学，直到整体缩放和旋转。它们都具有纯点动力学谱和连续本征函数，可从具有Rauzy分形型正则窗口的$4:2$维cut-and-project方案获得。任何Spectre铺砌的衍射测度也是纯点的。对于固定尺度和方向，改变形状MLD等价于仅改变投影方向。这些性质都源于第一Čech上同调尽可能小，没有留下改变动力学的形状变化空间。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The Spectre is an aperiodic monotile for the Euclidean plane that is truly chiral in the sense that it tiles the plane without any need for a reflected tile. The topological and dynamical properties of the Spectre tilings are very similar to those of the Hat tilings. Specifically, the Spectre sits within a complex $2$-dimensional family of tilings, most of which involve two shapes rather than one. All tilings in the family give topologically conjugate dynamics, up to an overall rescaling and rotation. They all have pure point dynamical spectrum with continuous eigenfunctions and may be obtained from a $4:2$ dimensional cut-and-project scheme with regular windows of Rauzy fractal type. The diffraction measure of any Spectre tiling is pure point as well. For fixed scale and orientation, varying the shapes is MLD equivalent to merely varying the projection direction. These properties all follow from the first \v{C}ech cohomology being as small as it possibly could be, leaving no room for shape changes that alter the dynamics.

  1. [2608.28774] 多变量加权形式
    On weighted forms in many variables
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究加权射影空间超曲面上整点数的渐近公式。
    • 利用圆法得到渐近公式。
    • 条件涉及变量数和奇异轨迹维数。
    • 验证积分Hasse原理。
    💡 用圆法证明加权射影空间中超曲面上整点数的渐近公式，验证Hasse原理。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文中，我们引入了几种利用圆法的新方法，以获得加权射影空间中超曲面上有界高度整点数的渐近公式。设$F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})$是变量$\mathbf{x} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_1}$和$\mathbf{y} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_2}$中次数为$d$的给定加权形式，其中变量$\mathbf{x}$和$\mathbf{y}$具有权重$w_1$和$w_2$，满足$w_1<w_2,\left(w_1, w_2\right)=1$，且$d>w_1 w_2$。记$$ R_F(P):=\#\left\{(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y}) \in \mathbb{Z}^{s_1+s_2}: F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0,|\mathbf{x}| \leq P^{w_1 / d},|\mathbf{y}| \leq P^{w_2 / d}\right\} . $$特别地，我们证明只要$$ s_1+s_2-\sigma_F>\left(1+\frac{w_2}{w_1}\right) \frac{d}{w_1} 2^{d / w_1}, $$其中$\sigma_F$是$F$的仿射奇异轨迹的维数，$R_F(P)$具有预期的渐近公式，即$$ R_F(P)=c P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}+o\left(P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}\right), $$其中$c$是局部密度的乘积。此外，只要$F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$在$\mathbb{R}$和每个素数$p$的$\mathbb{Q}_p$上有非奇异解，常数$c$为正。作为推论，我们验证了由$F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$定义的加权射影空间中足够大维数的拟光滑超曲面的积分Hasse原理。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we introduce several novel approaches utilizing the circle method to obtain the asymptotic formula for the number of integral points of bounded height lying on a hypersurface in a weighted projective space. Let $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})$ be a given weighted form of degree $d$ in variables $\mathbf{x} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_1}$ and $\mathbf{y} \in \mathbb{R}^{s_2}$, where variables $\mathbf{x}$ and $\mathbf{y}$ have weights $w_1$ and $w_2$ with $w_1<w_2,\left(w_1, w_2\right)=1$, and $d>w_1 w_2$. Write $$ R_F(P):=\#\left\{(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y}) \in \mathbb{Z}^{s_1+s_2}: F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0,|\mathbf{x}| \leq P^{w_1 / d},|\mathbf{y}| \leq P^{w_2 / d}\right\} . $$ In particular, we show that whenever $$ s_1+s_2-\sigma_F>\left(1+\frac{w_2}{w_1}\right) \frac{d}{w_1} 2^{d / w_1}, $$ where $\sigma_F$ is the dimension of the affine singular locus of $F$, the quantity $R_F(P)$ has the expected asymptotic formula, that is $$ R_F(P)=c P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}+o\left(P^{s_1 w_1 / d+s_2 w_2 / d-1}\right), $$ where $c$ is the product of local densities. Furthermore, the constant $c$ is positive whenever $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$ has a nonsingular solution over $\mathbb{R}$ and $\mathbb{Q}_p$ for every prime $p$. As a corollary, we verify the integral Hasse principle for the quasi-smooth hypersurface defined by $F(\mathbf{x} ; \mathbf{y})=0$ in a weighted projective space of sufficiently large dimensions.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28774

    The Cardy-Smirnov function of a four-marked planar domain encodes the conjectural scaling limit of the crossing probability for critical percolation. Kleban and Zagier later showed that on the rectangle the function admits a modular interpretation: it is determined by a modular functional equation together with a mild analytic ansatz.     📖 中文摘要

    受A. Schmidt关于复Lagrange谱低于$2$的部分的定理启发，我们研究由形如$\frac{1}{2}+i\alpha$（$\alpha\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathbb{Q}$）的复数被高斯有理数$p/q$（$p,q\in\mathbb{Z}[i]$，$q\neq 0$）逼近产生的受限Lagrange谱$L_{\frac{1}{2}+i\mathbb{R}}$。我们证明该谱允许用与实马蹄相关的动力学谱描述。作为推论，我们获得$L_{\frac{1}{2}+i\mathbb{R}}$的几个分形性质，例如维数函数$t\mapsto\dim_H(L_{\frac{1}{2}+i\mathbb{R}}\cap(-\infty,t))$的连续性。我们还证明满足\begin{equation*} \left\lvert z-\frac{p}{q}\right\rvert\geq\frac{1}{2|q|^2}, \quad\text{对所有 } p,q\in\mathbb{Z}[i], q\neq 0, \end{equation*}的复数$z$的集合是不可数的。事实上，我们证明该不等式对每个形如$z=\frac{1}{2}(1+i\theta)$的复数成立，其中$\theta\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathbb{Q}$是Schmidt的$C$-极小形式之一的根。

    🌐 English Abstract

Schmidt on the part of the complex Lagrange spectrum below $2$, we study the restricted Lagrange spectrum $L_{\frac{1}{2}+i\mathbb{R}}$ arising from the approximation of complex numbers of the form $\frac{1}{2}+i\alpha$, $\alpha\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathbb{Q}$ by Gaussian rationals $p/q$ with $p,q\in\mathbb{Z}[i]$, $q\neq 0$. In fact, we show that this inequality holds for every complex number of the form $z=\frac{1}{2}(1+i\theta)$ where $\theta\in\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathbb{Q}$ is a root of one of Schmidt's $C$-minimal forms.

  12. [2608.29587] 分圆Iwasawa伪测度的Bloch正则主部分
    Bloch-regulator Principal Parts of Cyclotomic Iwasawa Pseudomeasures
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 构造局部Iwasawa伪测度并研究其性质。
    • 证明伪测度模有界测度仅依赖Bloch类。
    • 给出主部分消失与K_3类消失的等价性。
    • 建立半平移Mellin分解并与GSWZ胚族比较。
    💡 构造局部Iwasawa伪测度，其模有界测度仅依赖Bloch类，并给出主部分消失的等价条件。
    📖 中文摘要

    设p为奇素数，K/Q_p为有限非分歧扩张。由p阶单位根的有限表示，我们在Z_p^×上构造局部Iwasawa伪测度。虽然伪测度依赖于所选表示，但其模有界测度的像仅依赖于相关的Bloch类：它是该类的Frobenius耗尽Coleman正则子乘以一个通用的半平移zeta主部分。因此，每个非例外权重分量有界，而例外分量至多有一个简单极点，其留数明确确定。对于p>3和Z_p值系数，主部分消失等价于K_3(K;Z_p)中对应类的消失。分圆细化保持Bloch类，并通过显式Iwasawa乘子作用于相关伪测度。细化的归一化有限线性组合插值有界权重空间数据的任意有限射流，仅受例外点归一化约束。我们还建立了半平移复Mellin分解，将构造与GSWZ胚族比较，并在素数p>3上方的简单一次点处推导出纽结5_2的局部K_3类的有限多对数准则。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $K/\mathbb{Q}_p$ be a finite unramified extension. From a finite presentation by roots of unity of order prime to $p$, we construct a localized Iwasawa pseudomeasure on $\mathbb{Z}_p^\times$. Although the pseudomeasure depends on the chosen presentation, its image modulo bounded measures depends only on the associated Bloch class: it is the Frobenius-depleted Coleman regulator of that class multiplied by a universal half-shifted zeta principal part. Consequently, every nonexceptional weight component is bounded, while the exceptional component has at most a simple pole with explicitly determined residue. For $p>3$ and $\mathbb{Z}_p$-valued coefficients, vanishing of the principal part is equivalent to vanishing of the corresponding class in $K_3(K;\mathbb{Z}_p)$. Cyclotomic refinements preserve the Bloch class and act on the associated pseudomeasures by explicit Iwasawa multipliers. Normalized finite linear combinations of refinements interpolate arbitrary finite jets of the bounded weight-space data, subject only to the normalization at the exceptional point. We also establish a half-shifted complex Mellin factorization, compare the construction with the GSWZ germ family, and derive, at simple degree-one places above primes $p>3$, a finite-polylogarithm criterion for the local $K_3$-class of the knot $5_2$.

    📖 中文摘要

Matching power sums of the slot constants through order $n$ forces the Type-$N$ product with binomial exponent $\binom{n+k-2}{n-1}$ to converge to a ratio of Vign\'eras multiple gamma values $\Gamma_n$; an analogue finder lifts any Type-1 evaluation to every higher type, and integer combinations of binomial exponents then realise arbitrary integer-valued polynomial exponents, yielding explicit products with exponents $k$, $k^2$, $k^3$, ... for constants such as $\pi/2$, $\sqrt{2}$, $e^{2K/\pi}$, and rational multiples of $\pi^{M!}$ (Part I). As the main application (Part II) we prove that for every positive integer $n$, a finite multiple-gamma template $\mathcal{S}_n$ with generalised Eulerian weights $T(n,k)$ (OEIS A225118) evaluates the Duke-Imamo\u{g}lu expression $\mathcal{D}_n = \beta'(-n) + (\log 4)\,\beta(-n)$. For odd $n$ this yields a convergent Wallis-Eulerian product for $e^{\beta'(-n)}$; for even $n$ the raw product diverges. The proof expands the template through the multiple-gamma functional equation, evaluates the quarter-integer coefficients in closed form, and identifies the resulting Eulerian-binomial sums with Duke's polynomials $P_{n+1,\ell}$.

    We compute the homological Euler characteristics of $\mathrm{SL}_4(\mathbb{Z})$ and $\mathrm{GL}_4(\mathbb{Z})$ with coefficients in arbitrary irreducible rational highest-weight representations. Applying Wall's formula, we combine the orbifold Euler characteristics of centralizers of torsion elements with traces computed using the Jacobi-Trudi identity to derive explicit formulas and rational generating functions. Consequently, these Euler characteristics are quasi-polynomial functions of the highest-weight parameters, of total degree at most two. We further derive degreewise vanishing results and parity-sensitive lower bounds for the dimensions of cohomology groups. The two extensions of an $\mathrm{SL}_4(\mathbb{Z})$-coefficient system to $\mathrm{GL}_4(\mathbb{Z})$ yield sharper bounds, which grow linearly or quadratically in explicit infinite families.   19. [2608.29812] 关于Drinfeld模的超奇异同源图
    On supersingular isogeny graphs of Drinfeld modules
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Drinfeld模的超奇异同源图。
    • 证明图的连通性。
    • 引入完备数E(p)并给出上下界。
    • 证实Micheli-Papikian猜想。
    💡 证明Drinfeld模超奇异同源图的连通性，并给出完备数E(p)的上下界。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究A=F_q[T]上秩二Drinfeld模的超奇异同源图。对于A的不同有限素理想p和q，我们证明特征p中边由循环q-同源给出的图是连通的。证明结合Gekeler的理想类对应与强逼近，将图实现为(F_q(T))_q^2中A_q-格子的位似类的Bruhat-Tits树的商。我们还引入完备数E(p)，即使得对于每个deg q≥E(p)的素数q≠p图完备的最小整数，并使用函数域上Brandt矩阵相关算子的特征值的Ramanujan-Petersson界推导显式上界。特别地，若d=deg p，则E(p)≤2d+2，且根据q和d的奇偶性有更精细的界。这证实了Micheli和Papikian的猜想：一旦deg q相对于deg p足够大，图就完备。我们进一步获得奇偶依赖的下界，并证明对于足够大的奇数d，E(p)≥d+log_q d-K（K为绝对常数），从而反驳了先前建议的界E(p)≤d+1。最后，我们证明一个准则，产生计算E(p)的算法。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study supersingular isogeny graphs of rank-two Drinfeld modules over $A=\mathbb{F}_q[T]$. For distinct finite primes $\mathfrak p$ and $\mathfrak q$ of $A$, we prove that the graph in characteristic $\mathfrak p$, with edges given by cyclic $\mathfrak q$-isogenies, is connected. The proof combines Gekeler's ideal-class correspondence with strong approximation to realize the graph as a quotient of the Bruhat--Tits tree of the homothety classes of $A_\mathfrak q$-lattices in $(\mathbb{F}_q(T))_\mathfrak q^2$. We also introduce the completeness number $E(\mathfrak p)$, the least integer such that the graph is complete for every prime $\mathfrak q\neq\mathfrak p$ with $\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak q\geq E(\mathfrak p)$, and derive explicit upper bounds using the Ramanujan--Petersson bound for the eigenvalues of operators associated to Brandt matrices over function fields. In particular, if $d=\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak p$, then $E(\mathfrak p)\leq 2d+2$, with sharper bounds depending on $q$ and the parity of $d$. This confirms a conjecture of Micheli and Papikian that the graph becomes complete once $\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak q$ is sufficiently large relative to $\operatorname{deg}\mathfrak p$. We further obtain parity-dependent lower bounds and prove that $E(\mathfrak p)\geq d+\log_q d-K$ for an absolute constant $K>0$ and sufficiently large odd $d$, thereby refuting the previously suggested bound $E(\mathfrak p)\leq d+1$. Finally, we prove a criterion yielding an algorithm to compute $E(\mathfrak p)$.

    💡 证明多维Lagrange谱的闭包等于0到上确界的区间，与一维情形不同。
    📖 中文摘要

    Lagrange谱是数论中的经典对象，定义为$\liminf_{q\to\infty} q\, \mathrm{dist}(q\alpha,\mathbb{Z})$的集合，其中$\alpha$遍历无理数。它具有复杂结构，包括离散部分、Hall射线和之间的过渡部分。类似地可以定义多维Lagrange谱，但至今对其了解不多。本文证明，与一维情形不同，多维Lagrange谱的闭包等于$0$到其上确界之间的区间。证明依赖于丢番图逼近与幺模格空间上的动力学之间的对应关系，并通过研究我们称为动力学Lagrange谱的动力学对应物来进行。后者通过利用设置的高秩性质，被证明等于$0$到其最大值之间的区间。从全动力学谱到丢番图谱的稠密性，通过应用格空间中扩张的horosphere平移的等分布来实现。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The Lagrange spectrum is a classical object in number theory, defined as the set of values of $\liminf_{q\to\infty} q\, \mathrm{dist}(q\alpha,\mathbb{Z})$ where $\alpha$ runs through irrational numbers. It has a complicated structure, with the discrete part, Hall's ray, and a transitional part in between. One can similarly define Lagrange spectrum in the multidimensional set-up, and until now not much has been understood about it. In this paper we prove that, unlike in the one-dimensional case, the closure of the multidimensional Lagrange spectrum is equal to the interval between $0$ and its supremum. The proof relies on a correspondence between Diophantine approximation and dynamics on the space of unimodular lattices and proceeds by studying a dynamical counterpart of the Lagrange spectrum that we call dynamical Lagrange spectrum. The latter is shown to be equal to the interval between $0$ and its maximum by means of an argument utilizing the higher rank nature of the set-up. A passage from full dynamical spectrum to the density of the Diophantine spectrum is achieved by applying equidistribution of expanding translates of horospheres in the space of lattices.

  34. [2608.30808] Galois环上APN置换的约化多项式提升及有效非APN界
    Reduced polynomial lifts of APN permutations over Galois rings and effective non-APN bounds
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 澄清APN提升猜想需要约化代表。
    • 给出提升的等价条件。
    • 证明非APN的显式阈值。
    • 证明三次置换多项式满足猜想。
    💡 澄清APN提升猜想，给出非APN的显式阈值。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们澄清了Rønjom和Sandrib关于Galois环的APN提升猜想。$\F_q$上的函数有许多多项式代表，其形式导数可能不同，因此猜想必须使用次数小于$q$的唯一约化代表；没有这种规范化，猜想是错误的。Galois环上的标准置换多项式准则给出精确约化：APN置换的约化代表$f$提升为$\GR(2^k,m)$（$k>1$）的置换当且仅当对每个$x\in\F_{2^m}$有$f'(x)\ne0$。因此修正的提升猜想等价于有限域临界点猜想。接下来我们使用Janwa--Wilson--Rodier曲面（通过对角线排列外的有理点编码APN条件）证明有效不存在性结果。对于每个奇数度$d\ge5$，除非是Gold指数$2^r+1$和Kasami--Welch指数$2^{2r}-2^r+1$，Hernando--McGuire和Aubry--McGuire--Rodier的结果在无穷远超平面截面中提供绝对不可约因子。这产生曲面的绝对不可约分量，定义在基域上且不包含在对角线排列中。显式Cafure--Matera估计给出可计算数$\APNmzero{d}$，使得当$m\ge\APNmzero{d}$时，$\F_{2^m}$上次数$d$的多项式不是APN。定性最终非APN结果归功于Aubry--McGuire--Rodier；我们的贡献是显式阈值。差分表的直接恒等式也给出偶数度推论：若$g$具有这样的奇数度，则$ax+g(x^2)+c$（$a\ne0$）与$g$具有相同的微分均匀性，因此在相同显式范围内不是APN。最后，我们直接证明每个三次置换多项式都有有理临界点，因此满足修正的提升猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We clarify the APN lifting conjecture over Galois rings of R\o njom and Sandrib (CCDS, 2026). A function on $\F_q$ has many polynomial representatives, whose formal derivatives may differ, so the conjecture must use the unique reduced representative of degree less than $q$; without this normalization, it is false. The standard permutation-polynomial criterion over Galois rings then gives an exact reduction: the reduced representative $f$ of an APN permutation lifts to a permutation of $\GR(2^k,m)$, $k>1$, if and only if $f'(x)\ne0$ for every $x\in\F_{2^m}$. Thus the corrected lifting conjecture is equivalent to a finite-field critical-point conjecture. We next use Janwa--Wilson--Rodier surfaces, which encode the APN condition by rational points off the diagonal arrangement, to prove an effective nonexistence result. For every odd degree $d\ge5$ outside the Gold exponents $2^r+1$ and Kasami--Welch exponents $2^{2r}-2^r+1$, results of Hernando--McGuire and Aubry--McGuire--Rodier provide an absolutely irreducible factor in the hyperplane section at infinity. This yields an absolutely irreducible component of the surface, defined over the ground field and not contained in the diagonal arrangement. The explicit Cafure--Matera estimate then gives a computable number $\APNmzero{d}$ such that no polynomial of degree $d$ over $\F_{2^m}$ is APN when $m\ge\APNmzero{d}$. The qualitative eventual non-APN result is due to Aubry--McGuire--Rodier; our contribution is the explicit threshold. A direct identity for difference tables also gives an even-degree consequence: if $g$ has such an odd degree, then $ax+g(x^2)+c$, with $a\ne0$, has the same differential uniformity as $g$ and is therefore not APN in the same explicit range. Finally, we prove directly that every cubic permutation polynomial has a rational critical point and hence satisfies the corrected lifting conjecture.

    Large zeta sums and zeros of the Riemann zeta function
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 建立大zeta和与零点之间的逆定理。
    • 证明零点计数下界。
    • 应用得到S(x,t)的上界。
    💡 证明大zeta和与zeta函数零点之间的逆定理。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于实数$t$和$x\ge 1$，设$S(x,t)=\sum_{n\le x} n^{\ii t}$。我们证明一个无条件逆定理，将$S(x,t)$的大值（$|t|$大）与高度$t$附近的Riemann zeta函数零点联系起来。更精确地，若$T\le |t|\le 2T$，$\exp(\sqrt{\log T})\le x\le \sqrt T$，且$|S(x,t)|=x/N$，其中$N\le (\log x)^{1/100}$，则对于每个$cN^6\le L\le (\log x)/2$，以$1+\ii\phi$为中心（$|\phi-t|\ll N$）的圆盘包含至少$L/360$个$\zeta(s)$的零点。作为推论，在$\Ree s=1$左侧任意薄固定窗口的短族中对零点的定量限制，在$x$的多项式范围内给出$S(x,t)\ll x/(\log x)^{1/100}$。证明改编了Granville和Soundararajan用于大特征和的零点强制机制。在zeta设置中，谱高偏移$t$，并且$\zeta(s)$极点的额外留数出现在高斯变换中；在考虑的范围内该留数指数小。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For real $t$ and $x\ge 1$, set \[ S(x,t)=\sum_{n\le x} n^{\ii t}. \] We prove an unconditional inverse theorem relating large values of $S(x,t)$, with $|t|$ large, to zeros of the Riemann zeta function near height $t$. More precisely, if $T\le |t|\le 2T$, $\exp(\sqrt{\log T})\le x\le \sqrt T$, and $|S(x,t)|=x/N$ with $N\le (\log x)^{1/100}$, then for every $cN^6\le L\le (\log x)/2$ a disk centered at $1+\ii\phi$, where $|\phi-t|\ll N$, contains at least $L/360$ zeros of $\zeta(s)$. As a consequence, a quantitative restriction on zeros in a short family of arbitrarily thin fixed windows to the left of the line $\Ree s=1$ yields $S(x,t)\ll x/(\log x)^{1/100}$ in polynomial ranges of $x$. The proof adapts the zero-forcing mechanism of Granville and Soundararajan for large character sums. In the zeta setting the spectral height is shifted by $t$, and an additional residue from the pole of $\zeta(s)$ appears in the Gaussian transform; in the range considered here that residue is exponentially small.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们建立了沿Kronecker流采样的球面可观测量的定量等分布估计。对于单调保测逆CDF参数化$\Phi_d:\mathbb{T}^d\to\mathbb{S}^d$和指数$\tau$的丢番图频率，每个$\varphi\in C^s(\mathbb{S}^d)$（$0<s\le1$）的时间平均以速率$O(T^{-\vartheta})$收敛到球面均值，其中$\vartheta=\frac{s}{(1+s)(\tau+d)}$，对初始相位和平均窗口一致。由于$\Phi_d$在极切割处不连续，证明将拉回局部化在极退化之外，并结合de la Vallée Poussin逼近、Fourier衰减和丢番图下界。对于连续保测变体，相同指数成立。相比之下，磨光后接Koksma--Hlawka给出，对每个$\varepsilon>0$，速率$O(T^{-s/[d(\tau+1)]+\varepsilon})$。其幂指数恰好当$\tau(d-1-s)<ds$时更大；在相等时名义指数一致，而局部化估计没有$T^\varepsilon$损失。在极退化附近消失的数据上，无截断论证给出指数$s/(\tau+d)$。我们将估计应用于收缩螺旋型轨迹、可积角数据和齐次奇点，包括幂律和指数径向收缩的对比。最后，在$\mathbb{S}^2$上，对于每个给定的$R(T)\to0$，我们构造一个有理独立频率和一个光滑零均值可观测$\varphi$（$\|\varphi\|_{C^1}\le1$），使得误差不是$O(R(T))$。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish quantitative equidistribution estimates for spherical observables sampled along Kronecker flows. For the monotone measure-preserving inverse-CDF parametrization $\Phi_d:\mathbb{T}^d\to\mathbb{S}^d$ and a Diophantine frequency of exponent $\tau$, every $\varphi\in C^s(\mathbb{S}^d)$, $0<s\le1$, has time averages converging to the spherical mean at rate $O(T^{-\vartheta})$, where $\vartheta=\frac{s}{(1+s)(\tau+d)}$, uniformly in the initial phase and averaging window. Since $\Phi_d$ is discontinuous across a polar cut, the proof localizes the pullback away from the polar degeneracies and combines de la Vall\'ee Poussin approximation, Fourier decay, and the Diophantine lower bound. The same exponent holds for a continuous measure-preserving variant. For comparison, mollification followed by Koksma--Hlawka gives, for every $\varepsilon>0$, the rate $O(T^{-s/[d(\tau+1)]+\varepsilon})$. Its power exponent is larger exactly when $\tau(d-1-s)<ds$; at equality the nominal exponents coincide, while the localization estimate has no $T^\varepsilon$ loss. On data vanishing near the polar degeneracies, the cutoff-free argument gives exponent $s/(\tau+d)$. We apply the estimates to shrinking spiral-type trajectories, integrable angular data, and homogeneous singularities, including a contrast between power-law and exponential radial contraction. Finally, on $\mathbb{S}^2$, for every prescribed $R(T)\to0$ we construct a rationally independent frequency and a smooth mean-zero observable with $\|\varphi\|_{C^1}\le1$ for which the error is not $O(R(T))$.

    💡 证明hyperkagome格子的格林函数是30级模函数。
    📖 中文摘要

    立方格子的格点格林函数是经典的：每个都是二阶算子的对称平方，具有完全椭圆积分的闭式。hyperkagome格子由自旋液体候选材料Na4Ir3O8的铱亚晶格实现，一直抵制这种处理：Varma和Monien将其态密度简化为三重积分，他们发现无法精确求解。从精确格点矩我们获得湮灭格林函数的不可约三阶线性微分（Picard-Fuchs）算子。它不是字面上的对称平方，但其对称平方具有有理解：微分Galois群是正交的，二阶微分交织器使算子射影地成为对称平方。我们的主要结果是底层二阶算子是$\Gamma_0(30)+$的亏格零模曲线的均匀化方程，$\Gamma_0(30)+$是由$\Gamma_0(30)$及其所有Atkin-Lehner对合生成的30级群，显式eta商参数化自然变量。该均匀化方程本身并不新——它是Lian-Yau表的亏格零群行——所以这里的新颖之处在于将格点格林函数与它识别，并给出证明：Schwarzian恒等式通过先验极点度界建立，而不是数值检查，这也证明了表中的条目。因此hyperkagome格林函数在30级是模的，提供了Varma和Monien寻求的闭式。其权重二周期显式获得：深度一拟模形式，我们证明它不是任何权重的模函数乘以代数函数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The lattice Green's functions of the cubic lattices are classical: each is a symmetric square of a second-order operator, with a closed form in complete elliptic integrals. The hyperkagome lattice, realized by the iridium sublattice of the spin-liquid candidate Na4Ir3O8, has resisted such a treatment: Varma and Monien reduced its density of states to a threefold integral they found no way to solve exactly. From exact lattice moments we obtain an irreducible third-order linear differential (Picard-Fuchs) operator annihilating the Green's function. It is not a literal symmetric square, yet its symmetric square carries a rational solution: the differential Galois group is orthogonal, and an order-two differential intertwiner makes the operator projectively a symmetric square. Our main result is that the underlying second-order operator is the uniformizing equation of the genus-zero modular curve of Gamma_0(30)+, the level-thirty group generated by Gamma_0(30) and all its Atkin-Lehner involutions, an explicit eta quotient parametrizing the natural variable. That uniformizing equation is itself not new - it is a row of the Lian-Yau table of genus-zero groups - so what is new here is the identification of a lattice Green's function with it, together with a proof: the Schwarzian identity is established by an a priori pole-degree bound rather than checked numerically, which also proves the tabulated entry. The hyperkagome Green's function is therefore modular at level thirty, supplying the closed form Varma and Monien sought. Its weight-two period is obtained explicitly: a depth-one quasimodular form which we prove is not a modular form times an algebraic function at any weight.

    🤖 AI 总结

    📖 中文摘要

    我们引入与有限图的Jacobian的特征相关的L-函数，作为代数曲线非分歧覆盖的L-函数的图论类比。这些L-函数使用图上的Riemann-Roch结构定义，并推广Lorenzini的二变量zeta函数。我们证明如果两个无桥图具有同构的Jacobian，且它们的L-函数在特征的诱导对应下一致，则它们的Laplacian格重合。我们还证明Lorenzini的zeta函数在收缩桥下不变，解释了主定理中无桥假设的必要性。最后，我们给出例子表明Jacobian或Lorenzini zeta函数单独都不能确定Laplacian格。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce $L$-functions associated with characters of the Jacobian of a finite graph, as a graph-theoretic analogue of the $L$-functions arising from unramified coverings of algebraic curves. These $L$-functions are defined using the Riemann--Roch structure on the graph and extend Lorenzini's two-variable zeta function. We show that if two graphs without bridges have isomorphic Jacobians and their $L$-functions agree under the induced correspondence of characters, then their Laplacian lattices coincide. We also show that Lorenzini's zeta function is invariant under contraction of bridges, explaining the necessity of the bridge-free hypothesis in the main theorem. Finally, we give examples showing that neither the Jacobian nor the Lorenzini zeta function alone determine the Laplacian lattice.

    Lattice point counting problems on step-two nilpotent Lie groups
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 本文研究二步幂零李群上的格点计数问题。
    • 方法上利用泊松求和、振荡积分和贝塞尔函数性质。
    • 主要结果是对齐次范数球给出显式计数误差估计，并在某些情形下达到尖锐界。
    • 该结果推广并改进了海森堡群上的已知结果，并扩展至任意维数中心。
    • 意义在于为更一般的幂零李群上的格点计数提供了统一框架。
    💡 该论文在二步幂零李群上建立了与抛物型膨胀和齐次范数相关的格点计数理论，给出了精确的计数误差估计，并改进了海森堡群上的已知结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们发展了连通单连通二步幂零李群上的格点计数理论，该群配备抛物型膨胀和一族适应膨胀结构的齐次范数 $\mathcal{N}_{\alpha,M}(x, t)=\left(|M_1x|^\alpha + |M_2t|^{\alpha / 2}\right)^{1 / \alpha}$，其中 $\alpha>0$，$M_1,M_2$ 为可逆矩阵。在适当的格概念下，待计数的区域是与这些范数相关的球，并对所有可能的维数和所有 $\alpha>0$ 推导出显式的计数误差估计。当群中心为一维且 $\alpha=2$ 时，这些界在某种有理意义下是尖锐的。我们的研究还推广并定量改进了 Garg--Nevo--Taylor 在海森堡群上的先前结果： (i) 在维数 5 中，若 $\alpha \in(3,4]$ 或 $\alpha=1$，对数因子的指数从 $2/3$ 降至 $1/3$；若 $\alpha\in(2,3]$，则去掉因子 $\log^{2/3} R$。 (ii) 在维数 3 中，对于 $\alpha=1$，估计从 $O_\epsilon(R^{5/2+\epsilon})$ 提升至 $O(R^{2}\log^{1/2} R)$；对于 $\alpha\in(1,2)$，提升至 $O(R^{19/8})$；对于 $\alpha>4$，去掉因子 $\log R$。此外，作为副产品，我们将最近由 Campolongo--Taylor 和 Srivastava--Taylor 考虑的海森堡球附近的格点计数推广到上述具有任意维数中心的二步群设置，并取得了一些定量改进。我们的方法依赖于泊松求和公式、振荡积分估计以及贝塞尔函数的渐近性质和递推公式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop the theory of lattice point counting on connected and simply connected nilpotent Lie groups of step-two, endowed with the parabolic type dilation and a family of homogeneous norms $ \mathcal{N}_{\alpha,M}(x, t)=\left(|M_1x|^\alpha + |M_2t|^{\alpha / 2}\right)^{1 / \alpha}$ adapted to the dilation structure, where $\alpha>0$ and $M_1,M_2$ are invertible matrices. With appropriate notions of lattices, the domains to be counted are balls associated to these norms, and explicit counting discrepancy estimates are deduced for all possible dimensions and all $\alpha>0$. The bounds are sharp when the group center is unidimensional and $\alpha=2$, in certain rational sense. Our study also generalizes and even quantitatively improves previous results on Heisenberg groups obtained by Garg--Nevo--Taylor \cite[\textit{Ann. Inst. Fourier}, 2015]{GNT15}: (i) In dimension $5$, the exponent of logarithmic factor is lowered from $2/3$ to ${1}/{3}$ if $\alpha \in(3,4] $ or $\alpha=1$; and the factor $\log ^{2/3} R $ is dropped if $\alpha\in(2,3]$. (ii) In dimension $3$, the estimation is upgraded from $O_\epsilon(R^{ 5/2+\epsilon})$ to $O(R^{2}\log^{ 1/2} R)$ for $\alpha=1$, and to $O(R^{{19}/{8}})$ for $\alpha\in (1,2)$; and the factor $\log R$ is removed for $\alpha>4$. Moreover, as a byproduct, we extend the lattice counting near Heisenberg spheres, recently considered by Campolongo--Taylor \cite[\textit{Matematica}, 2023]{CT23} and Srivastava--Taylor \cite[\textit{J. Fourier Anal. Appl.}, 2026]{ST26}, to the above step-two group setting with arbitrary dimensional group center, where some quantitative improvements are also attained. Our method relies upon Poisson's summation formulas, oscillatory integral estimates and asymptotic properties as well as recursion formulas of Bessel functions.

    🤖 AI 总结

    • 方法可能涉及数论和组合分析。
    • 这些结果加深了对幂的二进制表示的理解。
    💡 该论文证明了关于 $3^m$ 的二进制展开的精细结构：破坏周期性的位置数量以 $\log m/\log\log m$ 量级增长，且低阶位形成的有限词具有完全的低阶子词复杂度。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明了关于 $3^{m}$ 的二进制数字的精细结构的两个结果。首先，对于每个固定周期 $p$，$3^{m}$ 的二进制展开中破坏 $p$-周期性的位置数量以 $\log m/\log\log m$ 的量级增长；等价地，展开中深度超过 $\log m$ 的固定幂的窗口都不是 $p$-周期的。其次，由 $3^{m}$ 的低阶位形成的有限二进制词具有完全的低阶子词复杂度：一旦 $m$ 足够大，其复杂度函数满足 $p_{3^{m}}(n)\ge n+1$ 对所有长度 $n$ 成立。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove two results on the fine structure of the binary digits of $3^{m}$. First, for every fixed period $p$, the number of positions at which the binary expansion of $3^{m}$ breaks $p$-periodicity grows in order like $\log m/\log\log m$; equivalently, no window of the expansion deeper than a fixed power of $\log m$ is $p$-periodic. Second, the finite binary word formed by the low-order digits of $3^{m}$ has full low-order subword complexity: its complexity function satisfies $p_{3^{m}}(n)\ge n+1$ for every length $n$, once $m$ is large enough.

    • 证明了与 Hyodo--Kato 栈的关系。
    • 作为应用，证明了 Berthelot 猜想。
    • 该工作统一并推进了算术 $D$-模理论。
    💡 该论文定义并研究了特征 $p$ 域上概形的算术 de Rham 栈，给出了刚性上同调及其系数的栈论方法，并证明了 Berthelot 猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们定义并研究了特征 $p$ 域上概形 $X$ 的算术 de Rham 栈 $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$，并分析了它与相关栈（如 Hyodo--Kato 栈）的关系。我们证明 $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ 为刚性上同调及其系数（称为过收敛等晶体和算术 $D$-模）提供了栈论方法，避免了早期方法中长期存在的选取框架问题。最后，我们给出该形式体系的一些算术应用，例如证明了 Berthelot 关于光滑真推前保持过收敛等晶体的猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We define and study the arithmetic de Rham stack $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ of a scheme $X$ over a field of characteristic $p$, and analyze its relation with related stacks such as the Hyodo--Kato stack. We show that $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ gives a stack-theoretic approach to rigid cohomology and its coefficients, known as overconvergent isocrystals and arithmetic $D$-modules, which avoids the long-standing problem of frame-choosing in earlier approaches. We finally give some arithmetic applications of our formalism, such as a proof of Berthelot's conjecture on the preservation of overconvergent isocrystals by smooth proper pushforward.
